                                local generalHit = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("GeneralHit")
                                if generalHit then
                                    local localPlayer = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                                    for _, player in pairs(game:GetService("Players"):GetPlayers()) do
                                        if player ~= localPlayer and player.Character then
                                            local targetPart = player.Character:FindFirstChild("Right Arm") or 
                                                              player.Character:FindFirstChild("Left Arm") or
                                                              player.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") or 
                                                              player.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or 
                                                              player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                            if targetPart then
                                                generalHit:FireServer(targetPart)
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end)
                            task.wait(0.5)
                        end
                    end)
                    
                    Fluent:Notify({
                        Title = "Auto Receiver",
                        Content = "Auto Teleport + Slap Aura + Ability Enabled!",
                        Duration = 3
                    })
                    
                else
                    if receiverFullLoop then
                        task.cancel(receiverFullLoop)
                        receiverFullLoop = nil
                    end
                    
                end
            end
        })

        Tab:AddParagraph({
            Title = "Helper",
            Content = ""
        })

        local autoHelperSlap = false
        local helperSlapLoop = nil

        Tab:AddToggle("IndexForNothing", {
            Title = "Auto Teleport To Receiver + Slap Aura",
            Default = false,
            Callback = function(Value)
                autoHelperSlap = Value
                if Value then
                    local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                    local glove = player.leaderstats and player.leaderstats:FindFirstChild("Glove")
                    if not glove or glove.Value ~= "Default" then
                        Fluent:Notify({Title = "Error", Content = "Equip Default Glove first!", Duration = 3})
                        autoHelperSlap = false
                        return
                    end
                    local safespot = workspace:FindFirstChild("Safespot")
                    if not safespot then
                        Fluent:Notify({Title = "Error", Content = "Safespot not found! Create Safespot first.", Duration = 3})
                        autoHelperSlap = false
                        return
                    end
                    if helperSlapLoop then task.cancel(helperSlapLoop) end
                    helperSlapLoop = task.spawn(function()
                        while autoHelperSlap do
                            pcall(function()
                                local char = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
                                if char then
                                    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                    if hrp then
                                        local base = safespot:FindFirstChild("Base")
                                        if base then
                                            if (hrp.Position - base.Position).Magnitude > 10 then
                                                hrp.CFrame = base.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 10, 0)
                                            end
                                        else
                                            if (hrp.Position - Vector3.new(10000, -42, 9996)).Magnitude > 5 then
                                                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(10000, -42, 9996)
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                                local bRemote = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("b")
                                if bRemote then
                                    for _, player in pairs(game:GetService("Players"):GetPlayers()) do
                                        if player ~= game.Players.LocalPlayer and player.Character then
                                            local targetPart = player.Character:FindFirstChild("Right Arm") or player.Character:FindFirstChild("Left Arm") or player.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") or player.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                            if targetPart then bRemote:FireServer(targetPart) end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end)
                            task.wait(1.5)
                        end
                    end)
                    Fluent:Notify({Title = "Auto Helper", Content = "Auto Teleport + Slap Aura Enabled!", Duration = 3})
                else
                    if helperSlapLoop then task.cancel(helperSlapLoop) end
                end
            end
        })


    end

    -- ============================================================
    -- TAB: Fort
    -- ============================================================
    do
        local Tab = Window:AddTab({
            Title = "Fort",
            Icon = "rbxassetid://7734068321"
        })

        Tab:AddParagraph({
            Title = "1 Quest",
            Content = ""
        })

        Tab:AddButton({
            Title = "Get Badge + Plank Glove",
            Callback = function()
                local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                if player.leaderstats.Glove.Value == "Fort" and game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("entered") then
                    local hrp = player.Character.HumanoidRootPart
                    local originalPos = hrp.CFrame

                    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(8, 97, 4)
                    wait(0.2)
                    hrp.Anchored = true
                    wait(0.3)
                    game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Fortlol:FireServer()
                    wait(3.5)
                    hrp.Anchored = false
                    wait(0.1)
                    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(8, 106, -6)
                    wait(0.5)
                    hrp.CFrame = originalPos
                else
                    Fluent:Notify({ Title = "Error", Content = "Equip Fort Glove/Enter Arena", Duration = 3 })
                end
            end
        })

        Tab:AddParagraph({
            Title = "|||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||",
            Content = ""
        })

        Tab:AddParagraph({
            Title = "2-3 Quest",
            Content = ""
        })

        local autoFortDestroy = false
        local fortDestroyLoop = nil

        Tab:AddToggle("IndexForNothing", {
            Title = "Auto Teleport To Safespot + Auto Use Ability + Auto Destroy Fort Walls",
            Default = false,
            Callback = function(Value)
                autoFortDestroy = Value
                
                if Value then
                    local safespot = workspace:FindFirstChild("Safespot")
                    if not safespot then
                        Fluent:Notify({
                            Title = "Error",
                            Content = "Safespot not found! Create Safespot first.",
                            Duration = 3
                        })
                        autoFortDestroy = false
                        return
                    end
                    
                    if fortDestroyLoop then
                        task.cancel(fortDestroyLoop)
                        fortDestroyLoop = nil
                    end
                    
                    fortDestroyLoop = task.spawn(function()
                        while autoFortDestroy do
                            pcall(function()
                                local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                                local char = player.Character
                                
                                if not char then
                                    task.wait(0.5)
                                    return
                                end
                                
                                local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                if not hrp then
                                    task.wait(0.5)
                                    return
                                end
                                
                                local safespot = workspace:FindFirstChild("Safespot")
                                if safespot then
                                    local base = safespot:FindFirstChild("Base")
                                    if base then
                                        hrp.CFrame = base.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 10, 0)
                                    end
                                end
                                
                                task.wait(0.5)
                                
                                local fortlol = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Fortlol")
                                if fortlol then
                                    fortlol:FireServer()
                                end
                                
                                task.wait(0.5)
                                
                                local targetPart = nil
                                local targetColor = Color3.fromRGB(172, 73, 73)
                                
                                for _, part in pairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                                    if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Name == "Part" then
                                        local partColor = part.Color
                                        if partColor.R == targetColor.R and 
                                           partColor.G == targetColor.G and 
                                           partColor.B == targetColor.B then
                                            targetPart = part
                                            break
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                                
                                if targetPart then
                                    hrp.CFrame = targetPart.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
                                    
                                    task.wait(0.3)
                                    
                                    local selfKnockback = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("SelfKnockback")
                                    if selfKnockback then
                                        selfKnockback:FireServer({ 
                                            Force = 0, 
                                            Direction = Vector3.new(0, 0.01, 0) 
                                        })
                                    end
                                end
                            end)
                            
                            task.wait(4)
                        end
                    end)
                    
                    Fluent:Notify({
                        Title = "Auto Fort Destroy",
                        Content = "Auto Teleport + Ability + Destroy Fort Walls Enabled!",
                        Duration = 3
                    })
                    
                else
                    if fortDestroyLoop then
                        task.cancel(fortDestroyLoop)
                        fortDestroyLoop = nil
                    end
                    
                end
            end
        })


    end

    -- ============================================================
    -- TAB: Space
    -- ============================================================
    do
        local Tab = Window:AddTab({
            Title = "Space",
            Icon = "rbxassetid://7734068321"
        })

        Tab:AddParagraph({
            Title = "2 Quest",
            Content = ""
        })

        Tab:AddButton({
            Title = "Someone Using bus?",
            Callback = function()
                local found = false

                for _, player in ipairs(game.Players:GetPlayers()) do
                    local leaderstats = player:FindFirstChild("leaderstats")
                    if leaderstats then
                        local glove = leaderstats:FindFirstChild("Glove")
                        if glove and glove.Value == "bus" then
                            found = true
                            Fluent:Notify({
                                Title = "Detected!",
                                Content = player.Name .. " has equipped the bus glove.",
                                Duration = 3
                            })
                        end
                    end
                end

                if not found then
                    Fluent:Notify({
                        Title = "Error",
                        Content = "No one has equipped the bus glove",
                        Duration = 3
                    })
                end
            end
        })

        Tab:AddButton({
            Title = "Teleport To bus Player",
            Callback = function()
                local localPlayer = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                local foundPlayer = nil

                for _, player in pairs(game.Players:GetPlayers()) do
                    if player ~= localPlayer then
                        local stats = player:FindFirstChild("leaderstats")
                        if stats and stats:FindFirstChild("Glove") and stats.Glove.Value == "bus" then
                            foundPlayer = player
                            break
                        end
                    end
                end

                if foundPlayer and foundPlayer.Character and foundPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                    local hrp = foundPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart
                    localPlayer.Character:PivotTo(hrp.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0))

                    Fluent:Notify({
                        Title = "Success!",
                        Content = "Teleported To: " .. foundPlayer.Name,
                        Duration = 3
                    })
                else
                    Fluent:Notify({
                        Title = "Error",
                        Content = "bus Player Not Found",
                        Duration = 3
                    })
                end
            end
        })

        Tab:AddToggle("IndexForNothing", {
            Title = "Auto Bring Bus",
            Default = false,
            Callback = function(Value) 
                Giftfarm = Value
                
                task.spawn(function()
                    while Giftfarm do
                        local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                        if not player then 
                            task.wait(0.1) 
                            continue 
                        end
                        
                        local character = player.Character
                        if not character or not character.Parent then 
                            task.wait(0.01) 
                            continue 
                        end
                        
                        local hrp = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        if not hrp then 
                            task.wait(0.01) 
                            continue 
                        end
                        
                        for _, v in pairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                            if v.Name == "BusModel" and v:IsA("MeshPart") then
                                pcall(function()
                                    local originalCFrame = v.CFrame
                                    local originalAnchored = v.Anchored
                                    
                                    for _, child in pairs(v:GetChildren()) do
                                        if child:IsA("TouchInterest") then
                                            child:Destroy()
                                        end
                                    end
                                    
                                    v.Anchored = false
                                    v.CFrame = hrp.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 0, 5)
                                    task.wait(0.05)
                                end)
                            end
                        end
                        
                        task.wait(0.05)
                    end
                end)
            end
        })

        Tab:AddParagraph({
            Title = "|||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||",
            Content = ""
        })

        Tab:AddParagraph({
            Title = "3 Quest",
            Content = ""
        })

        Tab:AddButton({
            Title = "Teleport To Cannon Island",
            Callback = function()
                if game.Players.LocalPlayer.leaderstats.Glove.Value == "Space" then
                    game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(229, 47, 170)
                else
                    Fluent:Notify({ Title = "Error", Content = "Equip Space Glove", Duration = 3 })
                end
            end
        })

                end
            end
        })

        Tab:AddParagraph({

            Content = ""

                        return
                    end
                    if helperReceiverTeleportLoop2 then task.cancel(helperReceiverTeleportLoop2) end
                    helperReceiverTeleportLoop2 = task.spawn(function()
                        while autoHelperReceiverTeleport2 do
                            pcall(function()
                                local char = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
                                if char then
                                    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                    if hrp then
                                        local base = safespot:FindFirstChild("Base")
                                        if base then
                                            if (hrp.Position - base.Position).Magnitude > 10 then
                                                hrp.CFrame = base.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 10, 0)
                                            end
                                        else
                                            if (hrp.Position - Vector3.new(10000, -42, 9996)).Magnitude > 5 then
                                                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(10000, -42, 9996)
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end)
                            task.wait(1.5)
                        end
                    end)
                    Fluent:Notify({Title = "Auto Helper", Content = "Auto teleport to Receiver position enabled!", Duration = 2})
                else
                    if helperReceiverTeleportLoop2 then task.cancel(helperReceiverTeleportLoop2) end
                end
            end
        })


    end

        })

        })

        local autoReceiverSlapAura = false
        local receiverSlapAuraLoop = nil

        Tab:AddToggle("IndexForNothing", {
            Title = "Auto Teleport To Safespot + Slap Aura",
            Default = false,
            Callback = function(Value)
                autoReceiverSlapAura = Value
                
                if Value then
                    local safespot = workspace:FindFirstChild("Safespot")
                    if not safespot then
                        Fluent:Notify({
                            Title = "Error",
                            Content = "Safespot not found! Create Safespot first.",
                            Duration = 3
                        })
                        autoReceiverSlapAura = false
                        return
                    end
                    
                    if receiverSlapAuraLoop then
                        task.cancel(receiverSlapAuraLoop)
                        receiverSlapAuraLoop = nil
                    end
                    
                    receiverSlapAuraLoop = task.spawn(function()
                        while autoReceiverSlapAura do
                            pcall(function()
                                local char = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
                                if char then
                                    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                    if hrp then
                                        local safespot = workspace:FindFirstChild("Safespot")
                                        if safespot then
                                            local base = safespot:FindFirstChild("Base")
                                            if base then
                                                hrp.CFrame = base.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 10, 0)
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                                
                                local generalHit = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("GeneralHit")
                                if generalHit then
                                    local localPlayer = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                                    for _, player in pairs(game:GetService("Players"):GetPlayers()) do
                                        if player ~= localPlayer and player.Character then
                                            local targetPart = player.Character:FindFirstChild("Left Arm") or 
                                                              player.Character:FindFirstChild("Right Arm") or
                                                              player.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") or 
                                                              player.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or 
                                                              player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                            if targetPart then
                                                generalHit:FireServer(targetPart, 93.05674743652344)
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end)
                            task.wait(0.5)
                        end
                    end)
                    
                    Fluent:Notify({
                        Title = "Auto Receiver",
                        Content = "Auto Teleport + Slap Aura Enabled!",
                        Duration = 3
                    })
                    
                else
                    if receiverSlapAuraLoop then
                        task.cancel(receiverSlapAuraLoop)
                        receiverSlapAuraLoop = nil
                    end
                    
                end
            end
        })

        Tab:AddParagraph({
            Title = "Helper",
            Content = ""
        })

        local autoHelperRagdoll = false
        local helperRagdollLoop = nil

        Tab:AddToggle("IndexForNothing", {
            Title = "Auto Teleport To Receiver + Auto Die On Ragdoll",
            Default = false,
            Callback = function(Value)
                autoHelperRagdoll = Value
                if Value then
                    local safespot = workspace:FindFirstChild("Safespot")
                    if not safespot then
                        Fluent:Notify({
                            Title = "Error",
                            Content = "Safespot not found! Create Safespot first.",
                            Duration = 3
                        })
                        autoHelperRagdoll = false
                        return
                    end
                    if helperRagdollLoop then
                        task.cancel(helperRagdollLoop)
                        helperRagdollLoop = nil
                    end
                    helperRagdollLoop = task.spawn(function()
                        while autoHelperRagdoll do
                            pcall(function()
                                local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                                local char = player.Character
                                if not char or not char.Parent then
                                    char = player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
                                    task.wait(2)
                                    if not char then
                                        return
                                    end
                                end
                                local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                                if not hrp or not humanoid then
                                    task.wait(1)
                                    return
                                end
                                local hasEntered = char:FindFirstChild("entered")
                                if not hasEntered then
                                    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(-1209, 328, 3)
                                    task.wait(2)
                                    hasEntered = char:FindFirstChild("entered")
                                    if not hasEntered then
                                        task.wait(1)
                                        return
                                    end
                                end
                                local base = safespot:FindFirstChild("Base")
                                if base then
                                    if (hrp.Position - base.Position).Magnitude > 10 then
                                        hrp.CFrame = base.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 10, 0)
                                    end
                                else
                                    if (hrp.Position - Vector3.new(10000, -42, 9996)).Magnitude > 5 then
                                        hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(10000, -42, 9996)
                                    end
                                end
                                task.wait(1)
                                local ragdolled = char:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled")
                                if ragdolled and ragdolled.Value == true then
                                    humanoid.Health = 0
                                end
                            end)
                            task.wait(2)
                        end
                    end)
                    Fluent:Notify({
                        Title = "Auto Helper",
                        Content = "Auto Teleport + Auto Die On Ragdoll Enabled!",
                        Duration = 3
                    })
                else
                    if helperRagdollLoop then
                        task.cancel(helperRagdollLoop)
                        helperRagdollLoop = nil
                    end
                end
            end
        })


    end

                                            end
                                        end
                                    end

                                if abilityTimer >= 6 then
                                    abilityTimer = 0
                                    local sentry = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Sentry")
                                    if sentry then
                                        sentry:FireServer()
                                    end
                                end
                            end)

                                local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer

                                    char = player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
                                    task.wait(2)
                                    if not char then
                                        return
                                    end
                                end
                                local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                                if not hrp or not humanoid then
                                    task.wait(1)
                                    return
                                end

                                    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(-1209, 328, 3)

                                        task.wait(1)
                                        return

                                    end
                                end

                                local turret = workspace:FindFirstChild("Turret")
                                if turret then
                                    local hingePart = turret:FindFirstChild("HingePart")
                                    if hingePart then
                                        local glove = hingePart:FindFirstChild("Glove")
                                        if glove and (hrp.Position - glove.Position).Magnitude > 5 then
                                            hrp.CFrame = glove.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                                task.wait(1)
                                local ragdolled = char:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled")
                                if ragdolled and ragdolled.Value == true then
                                    humanoid.Health = 0
                                end
                            end)
                            task.wait(2)
                        end
                    end)
                    Fluent:Notify({
                        Title = "Auto Helper",
                        Content = "Auto Teleport + Auto Die On Ragdoll Enabled!",
                        Duration = 3
                    })
                else
                    if helperSentryLoop then
                        task.cancel(helperSentryLoop)
                        helperSentryLoop = nil
                    end
                end
            end
        })

                                task.wait(0.01)
                                
                                local phantomHit = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("PhantomHit")
                                if phantomHit then
                                    local localPlayer = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                                    for _, player in pairs(game:GetService("Players"):GetPlayers()) do
                                        if player ~= localPlayer and player.Character then
                                            local targetPart = player.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or 
                                                              player.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") or
                                                              player.Character:FindFirstChild("Right Arm") or
                                                              player.Character:FindFirstChild("Left Arm") or
                                                              player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                            if targetPart then
                                                phantomHit:FireServer(targetPart)
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end)
                            task.wait(0.5)
                        end
                    end)
                    
                    Fluent:Notify({
                        Title = "Auto Receiver",
                        Content = "Auto Teleport + Phantom Ability + Slap Aura Enabled!",
                        Duration = 3
                    })
                    
                else
                    if receiverPhantomLoop then
                        task.cancel(receiverPhantomLoop)
                        receiverPhantomLoop = nil
                    end
                    
                end
            end
        })

        Tab:AddParagraph({
            Title = "Helper",
            Content = ""
        })

        local autoHelperReceiverTeleport = false
        local helperReceiverTeleportLoop = nil

        Tab:AddToggle("IndexForNothing", {
            Title = "Auto Teleport To Receiver",
            Default = false,
            Callback = function(Value)
                autoHelperReceiverTeleport = Value
                if Value then
                    local safespot = workspace:FindFirstChild("Safespot")
                    if not safespot then
                        Fluent:Notify({Title = "Error", Content = "Safespot not found! Create Safespot first.", Duration = 3})
                        autoHelperReceiverTeleport = false
                        return
                    end
                    if helperReceiverTeleportLoop then task.cancel(helperReceiverTeleportLoop) end
                    helperReceiverTeleportLoop = task.spawn(function()
                        while autoHelperReceiverTeleport do
                            pcall(function()
                                local char = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
                                if char then
                                    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                    if hrp then
                                        local base = safespot:FindFirstChild("Base")
                                        if base then
                                            if (hrp.Position - base.Position).Magnitude > 10 then
                                                hrp.CFrame = base.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 10, 0)
                                            end
                                        else
                                            if (hrp.Position - Vector3.new(10000, -42, 9996)).Magnitude > 5 then
                                                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(10000, -42, 9996)
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end)
                            task.wait(1.5)
                        end
                    end)
                    Fluent:Notify({Title = "Auto Helper", Content = "Auto teleport to Receiver position enabled!", Duration = 2})
                else
                    if helperReceiverTeleportLoop then task.cancel(helperReceiverTeleportLoop) end
                end
            end
        })


    end

    -- TAB: Wormhole

    do
        local Tab = Window:AddTab({

        })

        Tab:AddParagraph({
            Title = "1-2-3 Quest",
            Content = ""
        })

        Tab:AddParagraph({
            Title = "Receiver",
            Content = ""
        })

        local autoReceiverWormhole = false
        local receiverWormholeLoop = nil

        Tab:AddToggle("IndexForNothing", {
            Title = "Auto Teleport To Safespot + Auto Use Ability + Slap Aura + Auto Teleport To Circle Portals",
            Default = false,
            Callback = function(Value)
                autoReceiverWormhole = Value
                
                if Value then
                    local safespot = workspace:FindFirstChild("Safespot")
                    if not safespot then
                        Fluent:Notify({
                            Title = "Error",
                            Content = "Safespot not found! Create Safespot first.",
                            Duration = 3
                        })
                        autoReceiverWormhole = false
                        return
                    end
                    
                    if receiverWormholeLoop then
                        task.cancel(receiverWormholeLoop)
                        receiverWormholeLoop = nil
                    end
                    
                    receiverWormholeLoop = task.spawn(function()
                        local portalTimer = 0
                        
                        while autoReceiverWormhole do
                            pcall(function()
                                local char = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
                                if char then
                                    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                    if hrp then
                                        local safespot = workspace:FindFirstChild("Safespot")
                                        if safespot then
                                            local base = safespot:FindFirstChild("Base")
                                            if base then
                                                hrp.CFrame = base.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 10, 0)
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                                
                                local wormholePlace = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("WormholePlace")
                                if wormholePlace then
                                    wormholePlace:FireServer()
                                end
                                
                                task.wait(0.1)
                                
                                local wormHit = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("WormHit")
                                if wormHit then
                                    local localPlayer = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                                    for _, player in pairs(game:GetService("Players"):GetPlayers()) do
                                        if player ~= localPlayer and player.Character then
                                            local targetPart = player.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or 
                                                              player.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") or
                                                              player.Character:FindFirstChild("Right Arm") or
                                                              player.Character:FindFirstChild("Left Arm") or
                                                              player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                            if targetPart then
                                                wormHit:FireServer(targetPart)
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                                
                                portalTimer = portalTimer + 0.5
                                if portalTimer >= 2 then
                                    portalTimer = 0
                                    
                                    local wormholes = workspace:FindFirstChild("Wormholes")
                                    if wormholes then
                                        local one = wormholes:FindFirstChild("One")
                                        local two = wormholes:FindFirstChild("Two")
                                        
                                        if one and two and hrp then
                                            if portalTimer == 0 then
                                                hrp.CFrame = one.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
                                            else
                                                hrp.CFrame = two.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end)
                            task.wait(0.5)
                        end
                    end)
                    
                    Fluent:Notify({
                        Title = "Auto Receiver",
                        Content = "Auto Teleport + Wormhole Ability + Slap Aura + Portal Teleport Enabled!",
                        Duration = 3
                    })
                    
                else
                    if receiverWormholeLoop then
                        task.cancel(receiverWormholeLoop)
                        receiverWormholeLoop = nil
                    end
                    
                end
            end
        })

        Tab:AddParagraph({
            Title = "Helper",
            Content = ""
        })

            Title = "Auto Teleport To Safespot + Slap Aura + Auto Use Ability + Auto Use Cherry's Clone Glove",
            Default = false,
            Callback = function(Value)
                autoReceiverCherry = Value

                            pcall(function()
                                local char = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
                                if char then
                                    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

                                                hrp.CFrame = base.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 10, 0)
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                                
                                local generalAbility = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("GeneralAbility")
                                if generalAbility then
                                    generalAbility:FireServer()
                                end
                                
                                task.wait(0.1)
                                
                                local generalHit = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("GeneralHit")
                                if generalHit then
                                    local localPlayer = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                                    for _, player in pairs(game:GetService("Players"):GetPlayers()) do
                                        if player ~= localPlayer and player.Character then
                                            local targetPart = player.Character:FindFirstChild("Left Arm") or 
                                                              player.Character:FindFirstChild("Right Arm") or
                                                              player.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") or 
                                                              player.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or 
                                                              player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                            if targetPart then
                                                generalHit:FireServer(targetPart)
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                                
                                cloneTimer = cloneTimer + 0.5
                                if cloneTimer >= 0.5 then
                                    cloneTimer = 0
                                    
                                    local cherryStorage = workspace:FindFirstChild("cherry_storage")
                                    if cherryStorage then
                                        for _, model in pairs(cherryStorage:GetChildren()) do
                                            if model:IsA("Model") and model.Name:lower():match("cherry") then
                                                local cloneRemote = model:FindFirstChild("clone_remote")
                                                if cloneRemote then
                                                    pcall(function()
                                                        cloneRemote:FireServer()
                                                    end)
                                                end
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end)
                            task.wait(0.5)
                        end
                    end)
                    
                    Fluent:Notify({
                        Title = "Auto Receiver",
                        Content = "Auto Teleport + Slap Aura + Ability + Cherry Clone Enabled!",
                        Duration = 3
                    })
                    
                else
                    if receiverCherryLoop then
                        task.cancel(receiverCherryLoop)
                        receiverCherryLoop = nil
                    end
                    
                end
            end
        })

        Tab:AddParagraph({
            Title = "Helper",
            Content = ""
        })

        local autoHelperCherry = false
        local helperCherryLoop = nil

        Tab:AddToggle("IndexForNothing", {
            Title = "Auto Teleport To Receiver + Auto Teleport To Cherry's Clone Glove + Auto Die On Ragdoll",
            Default = false,
            Callback = function(Value)
                autoHelperCherry = Value
                if Value then
                    local safespot = workspace:FindFirstChild("Safespot")
                    if not safespot then
                        Fluent:Notify({
                            Title = "Error",
                            Content = "Safespot not found! Create Safespot first.",
                            Duration = 3
                        })
                        autoHelperCherry = false
                        return
                    end
                    if helperCherryLoop then
                        task.cancel(helperCherryLoop)
                        helperCherryLoop = nil
                    end
                    helperCherryLoop = task.spawn(function()
                        while autoHelperCherry do
                            pcall(function()
                                local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                                local char = player.Character
                                if not char or not char.Parent then
                                    char = player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
                                    task.wait(2)
                                    if not char then
                                        return
                                    end
                                end
                                local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                                if not hrp or not humanoid then
                                    task.wait(1)
                                    return
                                end
                                local hasEntered = char:FindFirstChild("entered")
                                if not hasEntered then
                                    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(-1209, 328, 3)
                                    task.wait(2)
                                    hasEntered = char:FindFirstChild("entered")
                                    if not hasEntered then
                                        task.wait(1)
                                        return
                                    end
                                end
                                local base = safespot:FindFirstChild("Base")
                                if base then
                                    if (hrp.Position - base.Position).Magnitude > 10 then
                                        hrp.CFrame = base.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 10, 0)
                                    end
                                else
                                    if (hrp.Position - Vector3.new(10000, -42, 9996)).Magnitude > 5 then
                                        hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(10000, -42, 9996)
                                    end
                                end
                                task.wait(1)
                                local cherryStorage = workspace:FindFirstChild("cherry_storage")
                                if cherryStorage then
                                    for _, model in pairs(cherryStorage:GetChildren()) do
                                        if model:IsA("Model") and model.Name:lower():match("cherry") then
                                            local tool = model:FindFirstChild("Cherry")
                                            if tool then
                                                local glove = tool:FindFirstChild("Glove")
                                                if glove and glove:IsA("MeshPart") and (hrp.Position - glove.Position).Magnitude > 5 then
                                                    hrp.CFrame = glove.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
                                                end
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                                task.wait(1)
                                local ragdolled = char:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled")
                                if ragdolled and ragdolled.Value == true then
                                    humanoid.Health = 0
                                end
                            end)
                            task.wait(2)
                        end
                    end)
                    Fluent:Notify({
                        Title = "Auto Helper",
                        Content = "Auto Teleport + Cherry Clone Glove + Auto Die On Ragdoll Enabled!",
                        Duration = 3
                    })
                else
                    if helperCherryLoop then
                        task.cancel(helperCherryLoop)
                        helperCherryLoop = nil
                    end
                end
            end
        })


    end

                                        local safespot = workspace:FindFirstChild("Safespot")
                                        if safespot then
                                            local base = safespot:FindFirstChild("Base")
                                            if base then
                                                hrp.CFrame = base.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 10, 0)
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                                
                                local bonk = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("BONK")
                                if bonk then
                                    bonk:FireServer(-0.2956634000001941)
                                end
                                
                                task.wait(0.1)
                                
                                local generalHit = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("GeneralHit")
                                if generalHit then
                                    local localPlayer = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                                    for _, player in pairs(game:GetService("Players"):GetPlayers()) do
                                        if player ~= localPlayer and player.Character then
                                            local targetPart = player.Character:FindFirstChild("Left Arm") or 
                                                              player.Character:FindFirstChild("Right Arm") or
                                                              player.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") or 
                                                              player.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or 
                                                              player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                            if targetPart then
                                                generalHit:FireServer(targetPart)
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end)
                            task.wait(0.5)
                        end
                    end)
                    
                    Fluent:Notify({
                        Title = "Auto Receiver",
                        Content = "Auto Teleport + BONK Ability + Slap Aura Enabled!",
                        Duration = 3
                    })
                    
                else
                    if receiverBonkLoop then
                        task.cancel(receiverBonkLoop)
                        receiverBonkLoop = nil
                    end
                    
                end
            end
        })

        Tab:AddParagraph({
            Title = "Helper",
            Content = ""
        })

        local autoHelperBonk = false
        local helperBonkLoop = nil

        Tab:AddToggle("IndexForNothing", {
            Title = "Auto Teleport To Receiver + Auto Die On Ragdoll",
            Default = false,
            Callback = function(Value)
                autoHelperBonk = Value
                if Value then
                    local safespot = workspace:FindFirstChild("Safespot")
                    if not safespot then
                        Fluent:Notify({
                            Title = "Error",
                            Content = "Safespot not found! Create Safespot first.",
                            Duration = 3
                        })
                        autoHelperBonk = false
                        return
                    end
                    if helperBonkLoop then
                        task.cancel(helperBonkLoop)
                        helperBonkLoop = nil
                    end
                    helperBonkLoop = task.spawn(function()
                        while autoHelperBonk do
                            pcall(function()
                                local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                                local char = player.Character
                                if not char or not char.Parent then
                                    char = player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
                                    task.wait(2)
                                    if not char then
                                        return
                                    end
                                end
                                local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                                if not hrp or not humanoid then
                                    task.wait(1)
                                    return
                                end
                                local hasEntered = char:FindFirstChild("entered")
                                if not hasEntered then
                                    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(-1209, 328, 3)
                                    task.wait(2)
                                    hasEntered = char:FindFirstChild("entered")
                                    if not hasEntered then
                                        task.wait(1)
                                        return
                                    end
                                end
                                local base = safespot:FindFirstChild("Base")
                                if base then
                                    if (hrp.Position - base.Position).Magnitude > 10 then
                                        hrp.CFrame = base.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 10, 0)
                                    end
                                else
                                    if (hrp.Position - Vector3.new(10000, -42, 9996)).Magnitude > 5 then
                                        hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(10000, -42, 9996)
                                    end
                                end
                                task.wait(1)
                                local ragdolled = char:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled")
                                if ragdolled and ragdolled.Value == true then
                                    humanoid.Health = 0
                                end
                            end)
                            task.wait(2)
                        end
                    end)
                    Fluent:Notify({
                        Title = "Auto Helper",
                        Content = "Auto Teleport + Auto Die On Ragdoll Enabled!",
                        Duration = 3
                    })
                else
                    if helperBonkLoop then
                        task.cancel(helperBonkLoop)
                        helperBonkLoop = nil
                    end
                end
            end
        })


    end

                                v.BodyVelocity.Velocity = v.BodyVelocity.Velocity * _G.SetSpeedCloud

        local Players = game:GetService("Players")
        local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
        local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")
        local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
        local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

        local SelectedPlayer = nil
        local PlayersList = {}
        local ActiveToggles = {}

        local function RefreshPlayersList()
            PlayersList = {}
            for _, player in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if player ~= LocalPlayer then
                    table.insert(PlayersList, player.Name)
                end
            end
            return PlayersList
        end

        local function GetPlayerHitbox(playerName)
            local hitboxName = playerName .. "_hitbox"
            local hitbox = Workspace:FindFirstChild(hitboxName)
            if hitbox and hitbox:IsA("Part") then
                return hitbox
            end
            return nil
        end

        local function GetLocalPlayerHitbox()
            local hitboxName = LocalPlayer.Name .. "_hitbox"
            local hitbox = Workspace:FindFirstChild(hitboxName)
            if hitbox and hitbox:IsA("Part") then
                return hitbox
            end
            return nil
        end

        local function GetAnyHitbox()
            for _, obj in pairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do
                if obj:IsA("Part") and string.match(obj.Name, "_hitbox$") then
                    return obj
                end
            end
            return nil
        end

        local function FlyToTarget(targetHitbox)
            local character = LocalPlayer.Character
            if not character then return end
            
            local humanoidRootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not humanoidRootPart then return end
            
            for _, obj in pairs(humanoidRootPart:GetChildren()) do
                if obj:IsA("BodyVelocity") then
                    obj:Destroy()
                end
            end
            
            local bodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
            bodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            bodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(50000, 50000, 50000)
            bodyVelocity.Parent = humanoidRootPart
            
            local direction = (targetHitbox.Position - humanoidRootPart.Position).Unit
            local distance = (targetHitbox.Position - humanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude
            
            local speed = 50 + (distance * 2)
            bodyVelocity.Velocity = direction * speed
            
            local connection
            connection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                local currentDistance = (targetHitbox.Position - humanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude
                if currentDistance < 10 then
                    bodyVelocity:Destroy()
                    connection:Disconnect()
                    humanoidRootPart.CFrame = targetHitbox.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                end
            end)
            
            delay(3, function()
                if bodyVelocity and bodyVelocity.Parent then
                    bodyVelocity:Destroy()
                end
                if connection then
                    connection:Disconnect()
                end
            end)
        end

        local Dropdown = Tab:AddDropdown("IndexForNothing", {
            Title = "Select Player",
            Values = RefreshPlayersList(),
            Multi = false,
            Callback = function(Value)
                SelectedPlayer = Value
            end
        })

        Tab:AddButton({
            Title = "Refresh Players List",
            Callback = function()
                Dropdown:SetValues(RefreshPlayersList())
            end
        })

        local autoCloudLoop = false
        local cloudLoopThread = nil

        Tab:AddToggle("IndexForNothing", {
            Title = "Auto Teleport To Choosed Player's Cloud + Auto Jump From Cloud",
            Default = false,
            Callback = function(Value)
                autoCloudLoop = Value
                if Value then
                    if not SelectedPlayer or SelectedPlayer == "" then
                        Fluent:Notify({
                            Title = "Error",
                            Content = "Please select a player first!",
                            Duration = 3
                        })
                        autoCloudLoop = false
                        return
                    end
                    local playerExists = false
                    for _, player in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                        if player.Name == SelectedPlayer then
                            playerExists = true
                            break
                        end
                    end
                    if not playerExists then
                        Fluent:Notify({
                            Title = "Error",
                            Content = "Selected player no longer exists!",
                            Duration = 3
                        })
                        autoCloudLoop = false
                        return
                    end
                    if cloudLoopThread then
                        task.cancel(cloudLoopThread)
                        cloudLoopThread = nil
                    end
                    cloudLoopThread = task.spawn(function()
                        while autoCloudLoop do
                            local playerExists = false
                            for _, player in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                                if player.Name == SelectedPlayer then
                                    playerExists = true
                                    break
                                end
                            end
                            if not playerExists then
                                Fluent:Notify({
                                    Title = "Warning",
                                    Content = "Selected player left the game!",
                                    Duration = 3
                                })
                                autoCloudLoop = false
                                break
                            end
                            pcall(function()
                                local char = LocalPlayer.Character
                                if not char then
                                    return
                                end
                                local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                if not hrp then
                                    return
                                end
                                local cloudName = SelectedPlayer .. "_Cloud"
                                local cloud = Workspace:FindFirstChild(cloudName)
                                if cloud then
                                    local cloudPos = cloud.Position
                                    if (hrp.Position - cloudPos).Magnitude > 5 then
                                        hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(cloudPos + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0))
                                        task.wait(0.3)
                                        local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                                        if humanoid then
                                            humanoid.Jump = true
                                        end
                                    end
                                else
                                    for _, obj in pairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do
                                        if obj:IsA("BasePart") and string.find(obj.Name, SelectedPlayer) and string.find(obj.Name, "Cloud") then
                                            if (hrp.Position - obj.Position).Magnitude > 5 then
                                                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(obj.Position + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0))
                                                task.wait(0.3)
                                                local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                                                if humanoid then
                                                    humanoid.Jump = true
                                                end
                                            end
                                            break
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end)
                            task.wait(2)
                        end
                    end)
                    Fluent:Notify({
                        Title = "Cloud Helper",
                        Content = "Auto teleport to " .. SelectedPlayer .. "'s cloud + jump enabled!",
                        Duration = 3
                    })
                else
                    if cloudLoopThread then
                        task.cancel(cloudLoopThread)
                        cloudLoopThread = nil
                    end
                    Fluent:Notify({
                        Title = "Cloud Helper",
                        Content = "Auto teleport + jump disabled!",
                        Duration = 2
                    })
                end
            end
        })


    end

                                else
                                    if (hrp.Position - Vector3.new(10000, -42, 9996)).Magnitude > 5 then
                                        hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(10000, -42, 9996)
                                    end
                                end
                                task.wait(1)
                                local ragdolled = char:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled")
                                if ragdolled and ragdolled.Value == true then
                                    humanoid.Health = 0
                                end
                            end)
                            task.wait(2)
                        end
                    end)
                    Fluent:Notify({
                        Title = "Auto Helper",
                        Content = "Auto Teleport + Auto Die On Ragdoll Enabled!",
                        Duration = 3
                    })
                else
                    if helperSoulLoop then
                        task.cancel(helperSoulLoop)
                        helperSoulLoop = nil
                    end
                end
            end
        })


    end

            Default = false,

                    local safespot = workspace:FindFirstChild("Safespot")

                                    char = player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
                                    task.wait(2)
                                    if not char then
                                        return
                                    end
                                end
                                local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                                if not hrp or not humanoid then
                                    task.wait(1)
                                    return
                                end
                                local hasEntered = char:FindFirstChild("entered")
                                if not hasEntered then
                                    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(-1209, 328, 3)

                                        task.wait(1)
                                        return
                                    end
                                end
                                local base = safespot:FindFirstChild("Base")
                                if base then
                                    if (hrp.Position - base.Position).Magnitude > 10 then
                                        hrp.CFrame = base.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 10, 0)
                                    end
                                else
                                    if (hrp.Position - Vector3.new(10000, -42, 9996)).Magnitude > 5 then
                                        hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(10000, -42, 9996)
                                    end
                                end
                                task.wait(1)

                                if wellModel then
                                    local part = wellModel:FindFirstChild("Part")
                                    if part and part:IsA("BasePart") and (hrp.Position - part.Position).Magnitude > 5 then
                                        hrp.CFrame = part.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
                                    end
                                end
                            end)
                            task.wait(2)
                        end
                    end)
                    Fluent:Notify({
                        Title = "Auto Helper",
                        Content = "Auto Teleport + Pocket Dimension Portal Enabled!",
                        Duration = 3
                    })
                else
                    if helperWellLoop then
                        task.cancel(helperWellLoop)
                        helperWellLoop = nil
                    end
                end
            end
        })
