    -- ============================================================
    -- TAB: Bomb
    -- ============================================================
    do
        local Tab = Window:AddTab({
            Title = "Bomb",
            Icon = "rbxassetid://7734068321"
        })

        Tab:AddParagraph({
            Title = "1-3 Quest",
            Content = ""
        })

        Tab:AddToggle("IndexForNothing", {
            Title = "Auto Teleport To Safespot + Auto Use Ability + Auto Die On Ragdoll",
            Default = false,
            Callback = function(Value)
                if Value then
                    local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                    
                    if player.leaderstats.Glove.Value ~= "Bomb" then
                        Fluent:Notify({
                            Title = "Error",
                            Content = "Equip Bomb Glove",
                            Duration = 3
                        })
                        return false
                    end
                    
                    local safespot = workspace:FindFirstChild("Safespot")
                    if not safespot then
                        Fluent:Notify({
                            Title = "Error",
                            Content = "Create Safespot first!",
                            Duration = 3
                        })
                        return false
                    end
                    
                    getgenv().AutoBombTasksLoop = true
                    
                    task.spawn(function()
                        while getgenv().AutoBombTasksLoop do
                            pcall(function()
                                local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                                local char = player.Character
                                
                                if not char or not char.Parent then
                                    char = player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
                                    task.wait(1)
                                end
                                
                                if not char then
                                    task.wait(0.5)
                                    return
                                end
                                
                                local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                                
                                if not hrp or not humanoid then
                                    task.wait(0.5)
                                    return
                                end
                                
                                local hasEntered = char:FindFirstChild("entered")
                                
                                if not hasEntered then
                                    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(-1209, 328, 3)
                                    task.wait(1)
                                end
                                
                                local safespot = workspace:FindFirstChild("Safespot")
                                if safespot then
                                    local base = safespot:FindFirstChild("Base")
                                    if base then
                                        hrp.CFrame = base.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 10, 0)
                                    end
                                else
                                    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(10000, -42, 9996)
                                end
                                task.wait(1)
                                
                                local ragdolled = char:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled")
                                if ragdolled and ragdolled.Value == true then
                                    humanoid.Health = 0
                                    task.wait(1)
                                    return
                                end
                                
                                local bombThrow = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("BombThrow")
                                if bombThrow then
                                    bombThrow:FireServer("Ebutton")
                                end
                                task.wait(1)
                                
                                local bombFound = false
                                for i = 1, 30 do
                                    if not getgenv().AutoBombTasksLoop then break end
                                    
                                    for _, v in pairs(game.Workspace:GetChildren()) do
                                        if v.Name and string.find(v.Name, "_bømb") then
                                            hrp.CFrame = v.CFrame
                                            bombFound = true
                                            break
                                        end
                                    end
                                    
                                    if bombFound then
                                        break
                                    else
                                        task.wait(1)
                                    end
                                end
                                
                                ragdolled = char:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled")
                                if ragdolled and ragdolled.Value == true then
                                    humanoid.Health = 0
                                    task.wait(1)
                                    return
                                end
                                
                                if humanoid then
                                    humanoid.Health = 0
                                    task.wait(1)
                                end
                            end)
                            task.wait(0.5)
                        end
                    end)
                    
                    Fluent:Notify({
                        Title = "Auto Bomb Tasks",
                        Content = "Started! Teleporting to Safespot + Using Bomb",
                        Duration = 3
                    })
                    
                else
                    getgenv().AutoBombTasksLoop = false
                    
                end
            end
        })

        Tab:AddParagraph({
            Title = "|||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||",
            Content = ""
        })

        Tab:AddParagraph({
            Title = "1 Quest",
            Content = ""
        })

        Tab:AddButton({
            Title = "Throw Bomb/Explode Bomb",
            Callback = function()
                if game.Players.LocalPlayer.leaderstats.Glove.Value == "Bomb" then
                    game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").BombThrow:FireServer("Ebutton")
                else
                    Fluent:Notify({
                        Title = "Error",
                        Content = "Equip Bomb",
                        Duration = 3
                    })
                end
            end
        })


    end

    -- ============================================================
    -- TAB: Bubble
    -- ============================================================
    do
        local Tab = Window:AddTab({
            Title = "Bubble",
            Icon = "rbxassetid://7734068321"
        })

        Tab:AddParagraph({
            Title = "1-2-3-4 Quest",
            Content = ""
        })

        Tab:AddParagraph({
            Title = "Receiver",
            Content = ""
        })

        local autoReceiver = false
        local receiverLoopThread = nil
        local slapAuraConnection = nil
        local bubbleThrowTimer = 0

        Tab:AddToggle("IndexForNothing", {
            Title = "Auto Teleport To Safespot + Auto Use Ability + Slap Aura",
            Default = false,
            Callback = function(Value)
                autoReceiver = Value
                
                if Value then
                    if receiverLoopThread then
                        task.cancel(receiverLoopThread)
                        receiverLoopThread = nil
                    end
                    
                    receiverLoopThread = task.spawn(function()
                        while autoReceiver do
                            pcall(function()
                                local char = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
                                if not char then return end
                                local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                if not hrp then return end
                                
                                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(10000, -42, 9996)
                                task.wait(0.1)
                                
                                local bubbleThrow = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("BubbleThrow")
                                if bubbleThrow then
                                    bubbleThrow:FireServer()
                                end
                                
                                local bubbleHit = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("BubbleHit")
                                if bubbleHit then
                                    local startTime = tick()
                                    while autoReceiver and (tick() - startTime) < 6 do
                                        pcall(function()
                                            for _, player in pairs(game:GetService("Players"):GetPlayers()) do
                                                if player.Character then
                                                    local targetPart = player.Character:FindFirstChild("Right Arm") or 
                                                                      player.Character:FindFirstChild("Left Arm") or
                                                                      player.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") or 
                                                                      player.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or 
                                                                      player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                                    if targetPart then
                                                        bubbleHit:FireServer(targetPart)
                                                    end
                                                end
                                            end
                                        end)
                                        task.wait(0.5)
                                    end
                                end
                                
                                task.wait(1)
                            end)
                        end
                    end)
                    
                    Fluent:Notify({
                        Title = "Receiver",
                        Content = "Auto Receiver Enabled!",
                        Duration = 3
                    })
                    
                else
                    if receiverLoopThread then
                        task.cancel(receiverLoopThread)
                        receiverLoopThread = nil
                    end
                    
                    if slapAuraConnection then
                        slapAuraConnection:Disconnect()
                        slapAuraConnection = nil
                    end
                end
            end
        })

        Tab:AddParagraph({
            Title = "Helper",
            Content = ""
        })

        local autoHelper = false
        local helperLoopThread = nil

        Tab:AddToggle("IndexForNothing", {
            Title = "Auto Enter Arena + Auto Teleport To Receiver + Auto Teleport To Bubble Object + Auto Die When Bubble Object Dissapears",
            Default = false,
            Callback = function(Value)
                autoHelper = Value
                if Value then
                    if helperLoopThread then task.cancel(helperLoopThread) end
                    helperLoopThread = task.spawn(function()
                        while autoHelper do
                            pcall(function()
                                local char = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
                                if not char then return end
                                local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                if not hrp then return end
                                local hasEntered = char:FindFirstChild("entered")
                                if not hasEntered then
                                    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(-1209, 328, 3)
                                    task.wait(2)
                                    hasEntered = char:FindFirstChild("entered")
                                    if not hasEntered then task.wait(1) return end
                                end
                                if (hrp.Position - Vector3.new(10000, -42, 9996)).Magnitude > 5 then
                                    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(10000, -42, 9996)
                                    task.wait(0.5)
                                end
                                local bubbleFound = false
                                local bubblePart = nil
                                for _, obj in pairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do
                                    if obj:IsA("Model") and obj.Name == "BubbleObject" then
                                        for _, part in pairs(obj:GetDescendants()) do
                                            if part:IsA("BasePart") or part:IsA("MeshPart") then
                                                bubblePart = part
                                                bubbleFound = true
                                                break
                                            end
                                        end
                                    elseif obj:IsA("BasePart") or obj:IsA("MeshPart") then
                                        if obj.Name == "BubbleObject" or string.find(obj.Name, "Bubble") then
                                            bubblePart = obj
                                            bubbleFound = true
                                        end
                                    end
                                    if bubbleFound then break end
                                end
                                if bubbleFound and bubblePart and (hrp.Position - bubblePart.Position).Magnitude > 5 then
                                    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(bubblePart.Position + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0))
                                    task.wait(0.5)
                                end
                                local bubbleStillExists = false
                                for _, obj in pairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do
                                    if obj:IsA("Model") and obj.Name == "BubbleObject" then
                                        bubbleStillExists = true
                                        break
                                    elseif obj:IsA("BasePart") or obj:IsA("MeshPart") then
                                        if obj.Name == "BubbleObject" or string.find(obj.Name, "Bubble") then
                                            bubbleStillExists = true
                                            break
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                                if not bubbleStillExists then
                                    local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                                    if humanoid then humanoid.Health = 0 end
                                    task.wait(2)
                                else
                                    task.wait(1.5)
                                end
                            end)
                            task.wait(1.5)
                        end
                    end)
                    Fluent:Notify({Title = "Helper", Content = "Auto Helper Enabled!", Duration = 3})
                else
                    if helperLoopThread then task.cancel(helperLoopThread) end
                end
            end
        })


    end

            Title = "Receiver",

        })

        local slapAuraConnection = nil
        local shardAbilityTimer = 0

        Tab:AddToggle("IndexForNothing", {
            Title = "Auto Teleport To Safespot + Slap Aura + Auto Use Ability",
            Default = false,
            Callback = function(Value)
                autoReceiver = Value
                
                if Value then
                    if receiverLoopThread then
                        task.cancel(receiverLoopThread)
                        receiverLoopThread = nil
                    end
                    
                    receiverLoopThread = task.spawn(function()
                        while autoReceiver do
                            pcall(function()
                                local char = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
                                if not char then return end
                                local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                if not hrp then return end
                                
                                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(10225, -42, 9986)
                                task.wait(0.1)
                                
                                local shardsEvent = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Events")
                                if shardsEvent then
                                    local shards = shardsEvent:FindFirstChild("Shards")
                                    if shards then
                                        shards:FireServer()
                                    end
                                end
                                
                                local shardHit = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("ShardHIT")
                                if shardHit then
                                    local startTime = tick()
                                    while autoReceiver and (tick() - startTime) < 6 do
                                        pcall(function()
                                            for _, player in pairs(game:GetService("Players"):GetPlayers()) do
                                                if player.Character then
                                                    local targetPart = player.Character:FindFirstChild("Right Arm") or 
                                                                      player.Character:FindFirstChild("Left Arm") or
                                                                      player.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") or 
                                                                      player.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or 
                                                                      player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                                    if targetPart then
                                                        shardHit:FireServer(targetPart)
                                                    end
                                                end
                                            end
                                        end)
                                        task.wait(0.5)
                                    end
                                end
                                
                                task.wait(1)
                            end)
                        end
                    end)
                    
                    Fluent:Notify({
                        Title = "Receiver",
                        Content = "Auto Receiver Enabled!",
                        Duration = 3
                    })
                    
                else
                    if receiverLoopThread then
                        task.cancel(receiverLoopThread)
                        receiverLoopThread = nil
                    end
                end
            end
        })

        Tab:AddParagraph({
            Title = "Helper",
            Content = ""
        })

        local autoHelper = false
        local helperLoopThread = nil

        Tab:AddToggle("IndexForNothing", {
            Title = "Auto Teleport To Receiver + Auto Teleport To Shards Object",
            Default = false,
            Callback = function(Value)
                autoHelper = Value
                if Value then
                    if helperLoopThread then task.cancel(helperLoopThread) end
                    helperLoopThread = task.spawn(function()
                        while autoHelper do
                            pcall(function()
                                local char = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
                                if not char then return end
                                local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                if not hrp then return end
                                if (hrp.Position - Vector3.new(10000, -42, 9996)).Magnitude > 5 then
                                    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(10000, -42, 9996)
                                    task.wait(0.5)
                                end
                                local shotShard = workspace:FindFirstChild("ShotShard")
                                if shotShard then
                                    if shotShard:IsA("Model") then
                                        local targetPart = shotShard:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or shotShard:FindFirstChild("Head") or shotShard:FindFirstChild("Torso") or shotShard:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
                                        if targetPart and (hrp.Position - targetPart.Position).Magnitude > 5 then
                                            hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(targetPart.Position + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0))
                                        end
                                    elseif shotShard:IsA("BasePart") or shotShard:IsA("MeshPart") then
                                        if (hrp.Position - shotShard.Position).Magnitude > 5 then
                                            hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(shotShard.Position + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0))
                                        end
                                    end
                                else
                                    for _, obj in pairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
                                        if obj:IsA("BasePart") and string.find(obj.Name, "Shard") then
                                            if (hrp.Position - obj.Position).Magnitude > 5 then
                                                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(obj.Position + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0))
                                            end
                                            break
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end)
                            task.wait(1.5)
                        end
                    end)
                    Fluent:Notify({Title = "Helper", Content = "Auto Helper Enabled!", Duration = 3})
                else
                    if helperLoopThread then task.cancel(helperLoopThread) end
                end
            end
        })

    -- ============================================================
    -- TAB: Moon
    -- ============================================================
    do
        local Tab = Window:AddTab({
            Title = "Moon",
            Icon = "rbxassetid://7734068321"
        })

        Tab:AddParagraph({
            Title = "1-2-3 Quest",
            Content = ""
        })

        Tab:AddParagraph({
            Title = "Receiver",
            Content = ""
        })

        local moonGloveLoop = nil
        local moonSlapLoop = nil
        local moonRagdollLoop = nil
        local moonToggle = false

        Tab:AddToggle("IndexForNothing", {
            Title = "Auto Teleport To Safespot + Auto Ragdoll + Auto Equip/Unequip Glove + Slap Aura",
            Default = false,
            Callback = function(Value)
                moonToggle = Value
                
                if Value then
                    local safespot = workspace:FindFirstChild("Safespot")
                    if not safespot then
                        Fluent:Notify({
                            Title = "Error",
                            Content = "Safespot not found! Create Safespot first.",
                            Duration = 3
                        })
                        return false
                    end
                    
                    task.spawn(function()
                        while moonToggle do
                            pcall(function()
                                local char = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
                                if char then
                                    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                    if hrp then
                                        local safespot = workspace:FindFirstChild("Safespot")
                                        if safespot then
                                            local base = safespot:FindFirstChild("Base")
                                            if base then
                                                hrp.CFrame = base.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 10, 0)
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end)
                            task.wait(0.5)
                        end
                    end)
                    
                    moonRagdollLoop = game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                        if not moonToggle then return end
                        pcall(function()
                            if game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character and game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("entered") then
                                game.ReplicatedStorage.SelfKnockback:FireServer({
                                    ["Force"] = 0,
                                    ["Direction"] = Vector3.new(0, 0.01, 0)
                                })
                            end
                        end)
                    end)
                    
                    moonGloveLoop = game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                        if not moonToggle then return end
                        pcall(function()
                            local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                            local backpack = player:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
                            local character = player.Character
                            
                            if backpack and character then
                                local moonTool = backpack:FindFirstChild("Moon") or character:FindFirstChild("Moon")
                                if moonTool then
                                    local currentlyEquipped = character:FindFirstChild("Moon")
                                    if currentlyEquipped then
                                        moonTool.Parent = backpack
                                    else
                                        moonTool.Parent = character
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end)
                    end)
                    
                    task.spawn(function()
                        while moonToggle do
                            pcall(function()
                                local celestialHit = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("CelestialHit")
                                if celestialHit then
                                    local localPlayer = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                                    for _, player in pairs(game:GetService("Players"):GetPlayers()) do
                                        if player ~= localPlayer and player.Character then
                                            local targetPart = player.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") or 
                                                              player.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or
                                                              player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                            if targetPart then
                                                celestialHit:FireServer(targetPart)
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end)
                            task.wait(0.5)
                        end
                    end)
                    
                    Fluent:Notify({
                        Title = "Moon",
                        Content = "Auto Teleport + Ragdoll + Equip/Unequip + Slap Aura Enabled!",
                        Duration = 3
                    })
                else
                    if moonRagdollLoop then
                        moonRagdollLoop:Disconnect()
                        moonRagdollLoop = nil
                    end
                    if moonGloveLoop then
                        moonGloveLoop:Disconnect()
                        moonGloveLoop = nil
                    end
                    moonToggle = false
                end
            end
        })

        Tab:AddParagraph({
            Title = "Helper",
            Content = ""
        })

        Tab:AddToggle("IndexForNothing", {
            Title = "Auto Teleport To Receiver",
            Default = false,
            Callback = function(Value)
                if Value then
                    task.spawn(function()
                        while Value do
                            pcall(function()
                                local char = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
                                if char then
                                    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                    if hrp then
                                        local safespot = workspace:FindFirstChild("Safespot")
                                        if safespot then
                                            local base = safespot:FindFirstChild("Base")
                                            if base and (hrp.Position - base.Position).Magnitude > 10 then
                                                hrp.CFrame = base.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 10, 0)
                                            end
                                        else
                                            if (hrp.Position - Vector3.new(10000, -42, 9996)).Magnitude > 5 then
                                                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(10000, -42, 9996)
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end)
                            task.wait(1.5)
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
        })


    end

            Callback = function()
                if game.Players.LocalPlayer.leaderstats.Glove.Value == "Tycoon" then
                    local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                    local hrp = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    
                    if not hrp then
                        Fluent:Notify({
                            Title = "Error",
                            Content = "Character not found!",
                            Duration = 3
                        })
                        return
                    end

                    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, 0, 0)
                    task.wait(1)

                    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(17884, -24, -3539)
                    task.wait(1)

                    local args = {
                        [1] = CFrame.new(17893.552734375, -26.257728576660156, -3535.35888671875, 
                        0.00862560048699379, 3.3499091500743816e-08, -0.9999628067016602, 
                        8.43312697451637e-10, 1, 3.350761090814558e-08, 
                        0.9999628067016602, -1.1323045834288337e-09, 0.00862560048699379)
                    }

                    game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("GeneralAbility"):FireServer(unpack(args))
                    task.wait(1)

                    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, 0, 0)
                    
                    Fluent:Notify({
                        Title = "Success",
                        Content = "Tycoon placed at safe spot!",
                        Duration = 3
                    })
                else
                    Fluent:Notify({
                        Title = "Error",
                        Content = "Equip Tycoon Glove",
                        Duration = 3
                    })
                end
            end
        })


    end

    -- ============================================================
    -- TAB: 🗿
    -- ============================================================
    do
        local Tab = Window:AddTab({
            Title = "🗿",
            Icon = "rbxassetid://7734068321"
        })

        Tab:AddParagraph({
            Title = "1-2-3 Quest",
            Content = ""
        })

        Tab:AddParagraph({
            Title = "Receiver",
            Content = ""
        })

        local moaiToggle = false

        Tab:AddToggle("IndexForNothing", {
            Title = "Auto Teleport To Safespot + Auto Use Ability + Slap Aura",
            Default = false,
            Callback = function(Value)
                moaiToggle = Value
                
                if Value then
                    local safespot = workspace:FindFirstChild("Safespot")
                    if not safespot then
                        Fluent:Notify({
                            Title = "Error",
                            Content = "Safespot not found! Create Safespot first.",
                            Duration = 3
                        })
                        return false
                    end
                    
                    task.spawn(function()
                        while moaiToggle do
                            pcall(function()
                                local char = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
                                if char then
                                    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                    if hrp then
                                        local safespot = workspace:FindFirstChild("Safespot")
                                        if safespot then
                                            local base = safespot:FindFirstChild("Base")
                                            if base then
                                                hrp.CFrame = base.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 10, 0)
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                                
                                local generalAbility = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("GeneralAbility")
                                if generalAbility then
                                    local safespot = workspace:FindFirstChild("Safespot")
                                    if safespot then
                                        local base = safespot:FindFirstChild("Base")
                                        if base then
                                            local args = {
                                                [1] = base.CFrame
                                            }
                                            generalAbility:FireServer(unpack(args))
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                                
                                task.wait(14)
                                
                                local generalHit = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("GeneralHit")
                                if generalHit then
                                    local localPlayer = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                                    for _, player in pairs(game:GetService("Players"):GetPlayers()) do
                                        if player ~= localPlayer and player.Character then
                                            local targetPart = player.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") or 
                                                              player.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or
                                                              player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                            if targetPart then
                                                generalHit:FireServer(targetPart)
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end)
                            task.wait(0.5)
                        end
                    end)
                    
                    Fluent:Notify({
                        Title = "🗿",
                        Content = "Auto Teleport + Ability + Slap Aura Enabled!",
                        Duration = 3
                    })
                end
            end
        })

        Tab:AddParagraph({
            Title = "Helper",
            Content = ""
        })

        Tab:AddToggle("IndexForNothing", {
            Title = "Auto Teleport To Receiver + Auto Die On Ragdoll",
            Default = false,
            Callback = function(Value)
                if Value then
                    task.spawn(function()
                        while Value do
                            pcall(function()
                                local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                                local char = player.Character
                                if not char or not char.Parent then
                                    char = player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
                                    task.wait(2)
                                    if not char then
                                        return
                                    end
                                end
                                local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                                if not hrp or not humanoid then
                                    task.wait(1)
                                    return
                                end
                                local hasEntered = char:FindFirstChild("entered")
                                if not hasEntered then
                                    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(-1209, 328, 3)
                                    task.wait(2)
                                    hasEntered = char:FindFirstChild("entered")
                                    if not hasEntered then
                                        task.wait(1)
                                        return
                                    end
                                end
                                local safespot = workspace:FindFirstChild("Safespot")
                                if safespot then
                                    local base = safespot:FindFirstChild("Base")
                                    if base and (hrp.Position - base.Position).Magnitude > 10 then
                                        hrp.CFrame = base.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 10, 0)
                                    end
                                else
                                    if (hrp.Position - Vector3.new(10000, -42, 9996)).Magnitude > 5 then
                                        hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(10000, -42, 9996)
                                    end
                                end
                                task.wait(1)
                                local ragdolled = char:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled")
                                if ragdolled and ragdolled.Value == true then
                                    humanoid.Health = 0
                                end
                            end)
                            task.wait(2)
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
        })

end

    local ObbyReceiverLoop = nil

    Tab:AddToggle("IndexForNothing", {
        Title = "Auto Teleport To Safespot + Slap Aura (Receiver)",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(Value)
            ObbyReceiver = Value

            if Value then
                local safespot = workspace:FindFirstChild("Safespot")
                if not safespot then
                    Fluent:Notify({
                        Title = "Error",
                        Content = "Safespot not found! Create Safespot first.",
                        Duration = 3
                    })
                    ObbyReceiver = false
                    return
                end

                if ObbyReceiverLoop then
                    task.cancel(ObbyReceiverLoop)
                    ObbyReceiverLoop = nil
                end

                ObbyReceiverLoop = task.spawn(function()
                    while ObbyReceiver do
                        pcall(function()
                            local char = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
                            if char then
                                local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                if hrp then
                                    local base = safespot:FindFirstChild("Base")
                                    if base then
                                        if (hrp.Position - base.Position).Magnitude > 10 then
                                            hrp.CFrame = base.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 10, 0)
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end

                            local generalHit = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("GeneralHit")
                            if generalHit then
                                local localPlayer = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                                for _, player in pairs(game:GetService("Players"):GetPlayers()) do
                                    if player ~= localPlayer and player.Character then
                                        local targetPart = player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or 
                                                          player.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or
                                                          player.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
                                        if targetPart then
                                            generalHit:FireServer(targetPart)
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end)
                        task.wait(1.5)
                    end
                end)

                Fluent:Notify({
                    Title = "Obby Receiver",
                    Content = "Auto Teleport + Slap Aura Enabled!",
                    Duration = 3
                })

            else
                if ObbyReceiverLoop then
                    task.cancel(ObbyReceiverLoop)
                    ObbyReceiverLoop = nil
                end
            end
        end
    })

        Title = "Helper",
        Content = ""
    })

        Title = "Auto Farm Obby Task 2 (Hit 250 Players)",
        Default = false,

            ObbyTask2 = Value

                if ObbyTask2Loop then
                    task.cancel(ObbyTask2Loop)
                    ObbyTask2Loop = nil
                end

                        pcall(function()
                            local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer

                            task.wait(0.5)

                            if generalHit then
                                local closestPlayer = nil
                                local closestDistance = math.huge

                                for _, targetPlayer in pairs(game:GetService("Players"):GetPlayers()) do
                                    if targetPlayer ~= player and targetPlayer.Character then
                                        local targetHrp = targetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                        if targetHrp then
                                            local distance = (targetHrp.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
                                            if distance < closestDistance then
                                                closestDistance = distance
                                                closestPlayer = targetPlayer
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end

                                if closestPlayer and closestPlayer.Character then
                                    local targetPart = closestPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or
                                                      closestPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or
                                                      closestPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")

                                    end
                                end
                            end

                        end)

-- ============================================================
-- TAB: Voodoo
-- ============================================================
do
    local Tab = Window:AddTab({
        Title = "Voodoo",
        Icon = "rbxassetid://7734068321"
    })

    Tab:AddParagraph({
        Title = "1-2-3 Quest",
        Content = ""
    })

    Tab:AddParagraph({
        Title = "Receiver",
        Content = ""
    })

    -- Auto Teleport To Safespot + Auto Use Ability + Slap Aura Player/Voodoo Doll
    local autoReceiver = false
    local receiverLoopThread = nil

    Tab:AddToggle("IndexForNothing", {
        Title = "Auto Teleport To Safespot + Auto Use Ability + Slap Aura Player/Voodoo Doll",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(Value)
            autoReceiver = Value
            
            if Value then
                local safespot = workspace:FindFirstChild("Safespot")
                if not safespot then
                    Fluent:Notify({
                        Title = "Error",
                        Content = "Safespot not found! Create Safespot first.",
                        Duration = 3
                    })
                    autoReceiver = false
                    return
                end
                
                if receiverLoopThread then
                    task.cancel(receiverLoopThread)
                    receiverLoopThread = nil
                end
                
                receiverLoopThread = task.spawn(function()
                    while autoReceiver do
                        pcall(function()
                            local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                            local char = player.Character
                            
                            if not char or not char.Parent then
                                char = player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
                                task.wait(1)
                            end
                            
                            if not char then return end
                            
                            local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                            local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                            
                            if not hrp or not humanoid then
                                task.wait(0.5)
                                return
                            end
                            
                            local safespot = workspace:FindFirstChild("Safespot")
                            if safespot then
                                local base = safespot:FindFirstChild("Base")
                                if base then
                                    hrp.CFrame = base.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 10, 0)
                                else
                                    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(10000, -42, 9996)
                                end
                            else
                                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(10000, -42, 9996)
                            end
                            task.wait(0.1)
                            
                            local generalAbility = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("GeneralAbility")
                            if generalAbility then
                                generalAbility:FireServer()
                            end
                            
                            local generalHit = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("GeneralHit")
                            if generalHit then
                                local startTime = tick()
                                while autoReceiver and (tick() - startTime) < 4 do
                                    pcall(function()
                                        for _, targetPlayer in pairs(game:GetService("Players"):GetPlayers()) do
                                            if targetPlayer.Character then
                                                local targetParts = {
                                                    targetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Right Arm"),
                                                    targetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Left Arm"),
                                                    targetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Head"),
                                                    targetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso"),
                                                    targetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"),
                                                    targetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("DOLLPART")
                                                }
                                                
                                                for _, part in pairs(targetParts) do
                                                    if part then
                                                        generalHit:FireServer(part)
                                                    end
                                                end
                                                
                                                for _, child in pairs(targetPlayer.Character:GetChildren()) do
                                                    if child.Name == "DOLLPART" and child:IsA("BasePart") then
                                                        generalHit:FireServer(child)
                                                    end
                                                end
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end)
                                    task.wait(0.5)
                                end
                            end
                            
                            task.wait(0.5)
                        end)
                    end
                end)
                
                Fluent:Notify({
                    Title = "Receiver",
                    Content = "Auto Receiver Enabled! (Safespot + Ability + Slap Aura)",
                    Duration = 3
                })
                
            else
                if receiverLoopThread then
                    task.cancel(receiverLoopThread)
                    receiverLoopThread = nil
                end
            end
        end
    })

    Tab:AddParagraph({
        Title = "Helper",
        Content = ""
    })

    local autoHelper = false
    local helperLoopThread = nil

    Tab:AddToggle("IndexForNothing", {
        Title = "Auto Teleport To Receiver + Auto Die On Ragdoll",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(Value)
            autoHelper = Value
            if Value then
                if helperLoopThread then
                    task.cancel(helperLoopThread)
                    helperLoopThread = nil
                end
                helperLoopThread = task.spawn(function()
                    while autoHelper do
                        pcall(function()
                            local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                            local char = player.Character
                            if not char or not char.Parent then
                                char = player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
                                task.wait(2)
                                if not char then
                                    return
                                end
                            end
                            local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                            local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                            if not hrp or not humanoid then
                                task.wait(1)
                                return
                            end
                            if (hrp.Position - Vector3.new(10000, -42, 9996)).Magnitude > 5 then
                                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(10000, -42, 9996)
                                task.wait(0.5)
                            end
                            local ragdolled = char:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled")
                            if ragdolled and ragdolled.Value == true then
                                humanoid.Health = 0
                                task.wait(2)
                                return
                            end
                        end)
                        task.wait(2)
                    end
                end)
                Fluent:Notify({
                    Title = "Helper",
                    Content = "Auto Helper Enabled! (Receiver + Die On Ragdoll)",
                    Duration = 3
                })
            else
                if helperLoopThread then
                    task.cancel(helperLoopThread)
                    helperLoopThread = nil
                end
            end
        end
    })


end
