-- TAB: rob
-- ============================================================
do
    local Tab = Window:AddTab({
        Title = "rob",
        Icon = "rbxassetid://7734068321"
    })

    Tab:AddParagraph({
        Title = "1-2-3 Quest",
        Content = ""
    })

    Tab:AddParagraph({
        Title = "|||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||",
        Content = ""
    })

    Tab:AddParagraph({
        Title = "1 Quest",
        Content = ""
    })

    Tab:AddButton({
        Title = "Create Null Portal (Need Bob And Rob Gloves)",
        Callback = function()
            local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
            local hrp = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            
            if not hrp then
                Fluent:Notify({
                    Title = "Error",
                    Content = "Character not found!",
                    Duration = 3
                })
                return
            end
            
            hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new()
            task.wait(0.5)

            local robClick = workspace:FindFirstChild("Lobby") and workspace.Lobby:FindFirstChild("rob") and workspace.Lobby.rob:FindFirstChild("ClickDetector")
            if robClick then
                fireclickdetector(robClick)
            end
            task.wait(0.5)
            
            local robRemote = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("rob")
            if robRemote then
                robRemote:FireServer()
            end
            task.wait(0.5)

            local bobClick = workspace:FindFirstChild("Lobby") and workspace.Lobby:FindFirstChild("bob") and workspace.Lobby.bob:FindFirstChild("ClickDetector")
            if bobClick then
                fireclickdetector(bobClick)
            end
            task.wait(0.5)
            
            local bobRemote = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("bob")
            if bobRemote then
                bobRemote:FireServer()
            end

            Fluent:Notify({
                Title = "Done",
                Content = "Null Portal sequence completed!",
                Duration = 3
            })
        end
    })

    Tab:AddParagraph({
        Title = "|||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||",
        Content = ""
    })

    Tab:AddParagraph({
        Title = "2 Quest",
        Content = ""
    })

    local Walkspeed = 20
    local KeepWalkspeed = false

    Tab:AddSlider("IndexForNothing", {
        Title = "WalkSpeed",
        Description = "Set your walkspeed",
        Min = 20,
        Max = 1000,
        Default = 20,
        Rounding = 1,
        Callback = function(Value)
            Walkspeed = Value
            local char = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
            if char then
                local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if humanoid then
                    humanoid.WalkSpeed = Value
                end
            end
        end
    })

    Tab:AddInput("IndexForNothing", {
        Title = "WalkSpeed",
        Description = "Type a walkspeed value",
        Default = "20",
        Numeric = true,
        Finished = false,
        Callback = function(Value)
            local num = tonumber(Value)
            if num then
                Walkspeed = num
                local char = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
                if char then
                    local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if humanoid then
                        humanoid.WalkSpeed = num
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    })

    Tab:AddToggle("IndexForNothing", {
        Title = "Walkspeed Set Auto",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(Value)
            KeepWalkspeed = Value
            if Value then
                task.spawn(function()
                    while KeepWalkspeed do
                        pcall(function()
                            local char = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
                            if char then
                                local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                                if humanoid and humanoid.WalkSpeed ~= Walkspeed then
                                    humanoid.WalkSpeed = Walkspeed
                                end
                            end
                        end)
                        task.wait()
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
    })

    Tab:AddParagraph({
        Title = "|||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||",
        Content = ""
    })

    Tab:AddParagraph({
        Title = "3 Quest",
        Content = ""
    })

    local AutoAbsorb = false

    Tab:AddToggle("IndexForNothing", {
        Title = "Auto Absorb People",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(Value)
            AutoAbsorb = Value
            local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer

            if AutoAbsorb then
                if player.leaderstats.Glove.Value:lower() == "rob" then
                    task.spawn(function()
                        while AutoAbsorb do
                            pcall(function()
                                local players = game:GetService("Players"):GetPlayers()
                                local target = nil
                                local attempts = 0
                                
                                while not target and attempts < 20 do
                                    attempts = attempts + 1
                                    local randomPlayer = players[math.random(1, #players)]
                                    if randomPlayer ~= player and randomPlayer.Character and randomPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                                        target = randomPlayer
                                    end
                                end

                                if target then
                                    local targetHRP = target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                    local playerHRP = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                    
                                    if targetHRP and playerHRP then
                                        playerHRP.CFrame = targetHRP.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 0, 2)
                                    end

                                    local ability = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Ability")
                                    if ability then
                                        ability:FireServer()
                                    end
                                end
                            end)
                            task.wait(1)
                        end
                    end)
                else
                    AutoAbsorb = false
                    Fluent:Notify({
                        Title = "Error",
                        Content = "Equip Rob Glove",
                        Duration = 3
                    })
                end
            end
        end
    })

    local autoRobLoop = false

    Tab:AddToggle("IndexForNothing", {
        Title = "Auto Rob Transformation",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(Value)
            autoRobLoop = Value
            
            if Value then
                task.spawn(function()
                    while autoRobLoop do
                        pcall(function()
                            local robObject = workspace:FindFirstChild("Lobby")
                            if robObject then
                                robObject = robObject:FindFirstChild("rob")
                                if robObject and robObject:FindFirstChild("ClickDetector") then
                                    fireclickdetector(robObject.ClickDetector)
                                end
                            end
                            
                            task.wait(0.5)
                            
                            local robRemote = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("rob")
                            if robRemote then
                                robRemote:FireServer()
                            end
                        end)
                        task.wait(17)
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
    })


end

-- ============================================================
-- TAB: Tinkerer
-- ============================================================
do
    local Tab = Window:AddTab({
        Title = "Tinkerer",
        Icon = "rbxassetid://7734068321"
    })

    Tab:AddParagraph({
        Title = "1-2-3 Quest",
        Content = ""
    })

    Tab:AddParagraph({
        Title = "Receiver",
        Content = ""
    })

    local tinkererToggle = false

    Tab:AddToggle("IndexForNothing", {
        Title = "Auto Teleport To Safespot + Auto Use Ability + Slap Aura",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(Value)
            tinkererToggle = Value
            
            if Value then
                local safespot = workspace:FindFirstChild("Safespot")
                if not safespot then
                    Fluent:Notify({
                        Title = "Error",
                        Content = "Safespot not found! Create Safespot first.",
                        Duration = 3
                    })
                    return false
                end
                
                task.spawn(function()
                    while tinkererToggle do
                        pcall(function()
                            local char = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
                            if char then
                                local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                if hrp then
                                    local safespot = workspace:FindFirstChild("Safespot")
                                    if safespot then
                                        local base = safespot:FindFirstChild("Base")
                                        if base then
                                            hrp.CFrame = base.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 10, 0)
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                            
                            local generalAbility = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("GeneralAbility")
                            if generalAbility then
                                generalAbility:FireServer()
                            end
                            
                            task.wait(7)
                            
                            local tinkererHit = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("TinkererHit")
                            if tinkererHit then
                                local localPlayer = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                                for _, player in pairs(game:GetService("Players"):GetPlayers()) do
                                    if player ~= localPlayer and player.Character then
                                        local targetPart = player.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") or 
                                                          player.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or
                                                          player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                        if targetPart then
                                            tinkererHit:FireServer(targetPart)
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end)
                        task.wait(0.5)
                    end
                end)
                
                Fluent:Notify({
                    Title = "Tinkerer",
                    Content = "Auto Teleport + Ability + Slap Aura Enabled!",
                    Duration = 3
                })
            end
        end
    })

    Tab:AddParagraph({
        Title = "Helper",
        Content = ""
    })

    Tab:AddToggle("IndexForNothing", {
        Title = "Auto Teleport To Receiver + Auto Die On Ragdoll",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(Value)
            if Value then
                task.spawn(function()
                    while Value do
                        pcall(function()
                            local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                            local char = player.Character
                            if not char or not char.Parent then
                                char = player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
                                task.wait(2)
                                if not char then
                                    return
                                end
                            end
                            local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                            local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                            if not hrp or not humanoid then
                                task.wait(1)
                                return
                            end
                            local hasEntered = char:FindFirstChild("entered")
                            if not hasEntered then
                                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(-1209, 328, 3)
                                task.wait(2)
                                hasEntered = char:FindFirstChild("entered")
                                if not hasEntered then
                                    task.wait(1)
                                    return
                                end
                            end
                            local safespot = workspace:FindFirstChild("Safespot")
                            if safespot then
                                local base = safespot:FindFirstChild("Base")
                                if base and (hrp.Position - base.Position).Magnitude > 10 then
                                    hrp.CFrame = base.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 10, 0)
                                end
                            else
                                if (hrp.Position - Vector3.new(10000, -42, 9996)).Magnitude > 5 then
                                    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(10000, -42, 9996)
                                end
                            end
                            task.wait(1)
                            local ragdolled = char:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled")
                            if ragdolled and ragdolled.Value == true then
                                humanoid.Health = 0
                            end
                        end)
                        task.wait(2)
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
    })


end

-- ============================================================
-- TAB: Firework
-- ============================================================
do
    local Tab = Window:AddTab({
        Title = "Firework",
        Icon = "rbxassetid://7734068321"
    })

    Tab:AddParagraph({
        Title = "1-2-3 Quest",
        Content = ""
    })

    Tab:AddParagraph({
        Title = "Receiver",
        Content = ""
    })

    local autoReceiver = false
    local receiverLoop = nil
    local slapAuraConnection = nil
    local abilityConnection = nil
    local fireworkConnection = nil
    local jumpTimer = 0
    local abilityTimer = 0
    local slapTimer = 0
    local fireworkTimer = 0

    Tab:AddToggle("IndexForNothing", {
        Title = "Auto Receiver (Safespot + Ability + Firework + Jump + Slap Aura)",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(Value)
            autoReceiver = Value
            
            if Value then
                jumpTimer = 0
                abilityTimer = 0
                slapTimer = 0
                fireworkTimer = 0
                
                if receiverLoop then
                    task.cancel(receiverLoop)
                    receiverLoop = nil
                end
                
                receiverLoop = task.spawn(function()
                    local jumpTimer = 0
                    
                    while autoReceiver do
                        pcall(function()
                            local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                            local char = player.Character
                            if not char then return end
                            
                            local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                            if not hrp then return end
                            
                            local safespot = workspace:FindFirstChild("Safespot")
                            if safespot then
                                local base = safespot:FindFirstChild("Base")
                                if base and base:IsA("BasePart") then
                                    hrp.CFrame = base.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 10, 0)
                                end
                            end
                            
                            jumpTimer = jumpTimer + 0.01
                            if jumpTimer >= 4 then
                                local humanoid = char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                                if humanoid then
                                    humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
                                end
                                jumpTimer = 0
                            end
                        end)
                        task.wait(0.01)
                    end
                end)
                
                if fireworkConnection then
                    fireworkConnection:Disconnect()
                    fireworkConnection = nil
                end
                
                fireworkConnection = game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat:Connect(function(deltaTime)
                    if not autoReceiver then 
                        fireworkConnection:Disconnect()
                        fireworkConnection = nil
                        return 
                    end
                    
                    fireworkTimer = fireworkTimer + deltaTime
                    if fireworkTimer >= 1 then
                        pcall(function()
                            local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                            local char = player.Character
                            if not char then return end
                            
                            local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                            if not hrp then return end
                            
                            local playerName = player.Name
                            local fireworkPart = workspace:FindFirstChild("Firework_" .. playerName)
                            if fireworkPart and fireworkPart:IsA("BasePart") then
                                hrp.CFrame = fireworkPart.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0)
                            end
                        end)
                        fireworkTimer = 0
                    end
                end)
                
                if abilityConnection then
                    abilityConnection:Disconnect()
                    abilityConnection = nil
                end
                
                abilityConnection = game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat:Connect(function(deltaTime)
                    if not autoReceiver then 
                        abilityConnection:Disconnect()
                        abilityConnection = nil
                        return 
                    end
                    
                    abilityTimer = abilityTimer + deltaTime
                    if abilityTimer >= 3 then
                        pcall(function()
                            local generalAbility = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("GeneralAbility")
                            if generalAbility then
                                generalAbility:FireServer()
                            end
                        end)
                        abilityTimer = 0
                    end
                end)
                
                if slapAuraConnection then
                    slapAuraConnection:Disconnect()
                    slapAuraConnection = nil
                end
                
                slapAuraConnection = game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat:Connect(function(deltaTime)
                    if not autoReceiver then 
                        slapAuraConnection:Disconnect()
                        slapAuraConnection = nil
                        return 
                    end
                    
                    slapTimer = slapTimer + deltaTime
                    if slapTimer >= 3 then
                        pcall(function()
                            local generalHit = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("GeneralHit")
                            if not generalHit then return end
                            
                            for _, player in pairs(game:GetService("Players"):GetPlayers()) do
                                if player ~= game.Players.LocalPlayer and player.Character then
                                    local targetPart = player.Character:FindFirstChild("Right Arm") or 
                                                      player.Character:FindFirstChild("Left Arm") or
                                                      player.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") or 
                                                      player.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or 
                                                      player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                    if targetPart then
                                        generalHit:FireServer(targetPart)
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end)
                        slapTimer = 0
                    end
                end)
                
                Fluent:Notify({
                    Title = "Auto Receiver",
                    Content = "Enabled! Safespot at 0.01s, Firework at 1s, others at 3s.",
                    Duration = 3
                })
                
            else
                if receiverLoop then
                    task.cancel(receiverLoop)
                    receiverLoop = nil
                end
                
                if fireworkConnection then
                    fireworkConnection:Disconnect()
                    fireworkConnection = nil
                end
                
                if abilityConnection then
                    abilityConnection:Disconnect()
                    abilityConnection = nil
                end
                
                if slapAuraConnection then
                    slapAuraConnection:Disconnect()
                    slapAuraConnection = nil
                end
                
                jumpTimer = 0
                abilityTimer = 0
                slapTimer = 0
                fireworkTimer = 0
            end
        end
    })

    Tab:AddParagraph({
        Title = "Helper",
        Content = ""
    })

    local autoHelper = false
    local helperLoop = nil
    local helperJumpTimer = 0

    Tab:AddToggle("IndexForNothing", {
        Title = "Auto Helper (Safespot + Firework + Jump + Die on Ragdoll + Auto Re-enter)",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(Value)
            autoHelper = Value
            if Value then
                helperJumpTimer = 0
                if helperLoop then task.cancel(helperLoop) end
                helperLoop = task.spawn(function()
                    while autoHelper do
                        pcall(function()
                            local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                            local char = player.Character
                            if not char then task.wait(1) return end
                            local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                            if not hrp then task.wait(1) return end
                            if (hrp.Position - Vector3.new(10000, -42, 9996)).Magnitude > 5 then
                                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(10000, -42, 9996)
                                task.wait(0.5)
                            end
                            for _, part in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
                                if part:IsA("BasePart") and string.find(part.Name, "Firework") then
                                    if (hrp.Position - part.Position).Magnitude > 5 then
                                        hrp.CFrame = part.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0)
                                    end
                                    break
                                end
                            end
                            helperJumpTimer = helperJumpTimer + 1
                            if helperJumpTimer >= 4 then
                                local humanoid = char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                                if humanoid then
                                    humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
                                end
                                helperJumpTimer = 0
                            end
                            local entered = char:FindFirstChild("entered")
                            if entered == nil then
                                if (hrp.Position - Vector3.new(-1209, 328, 3)).Magnitude > 5 then
                                    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(-1209, 328, 3)
                                end
                                task.wait(1)
                            else
                                local ragdolled = char:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled")
                                if ragdolled and ragdolled.Value == true then
                                    local humanoid = char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                                    if humanoid then
                                        humanoid.Health = 0
                                        task.wait(3)
                                        local newChar = player.Character
                                        if newChar then
                                            local newHrp = newChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                            if newHrp and (newHrp.Position - Vector3.new(-1209, 328, 3)).Magnitude > 5 then
                                                newHrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(-1209, 328, 3)
                                            end
                                            task.wait(1)
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end)
                        task.wait(1.5)
                    end
                end)
                Fluent:Notify({Title = "Auto Helper", Content = "Enabled! Teleporting to Safespot, Firework, Jumping & monitoring ragdoll.", Duration = 3})
            else
                if helperLoop then task.cancel(helperLoop) end
                helperJumpTimer = 0
            end
        end
    })


end

    local Tab = Window:AddTab({

        Title = "Auto Teleport To Safespot + Auto Use Ability + Slap Aura",

            glovelToggle = Value

                local safespot = workspace:FindFirstChild("Safespot")

-- ============================================================
-- TAB: Run
-- ============================================================
do
    local Tab = Window:AddTab({
        Title = "Run",
        Icon = "rbxassetid://7734068321"
    })

    Tab:AddParagraph({
        Title = "1 Quest",
        Content = ""
    })

    local autoLabyrinthKill = false
    local autoLabyrinthLoop = nil

    Tab:AddToggle("IndexForNothing", {
        Title = "Auto Kill Players In Labyrinth",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(Value)
            autoLabyrinthKill = Value

            if Value then
                local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                local char = player.Character
                
                if not char then
                    Fluent:Notify({
                        Title = "Error",
                        Content = "Character not found!",
                        Duration = 3
                    })
                    autoLabyrinthKill = false
                    return
                end
                
                local hasInLabyrinth = char:FindFirstChild("InLabyrinth")
                if not hasInLabyrinth then
                    Fluent:Notify({
                        Title = "Error",
                        Content = "You are not in Labyrinth!",
                        Duration = 3
                    })
                    autoLabyrinthKill = false
                    return
                end

                if autoLabyrinthLoop then
                    task.cancel(autoLabyrinthLoop)
                    autoLabyrinthLoop = nil
                end

                autoLabyrinthLoop = task.spawn(function()
                    while autoLabyrinthKill do
                        local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                        local char = player.Character
                        
                        if not char or not char:FindFirstChild("InLabyrinth") then
                            Fluent:Notify({
                                Title = "Stopped",
                                Content = "You are no longer in Labyrinth!",
                                Duration = 3
                            })
                            autoLabyrinthKill = false
                            break
                        end
                        
                        pcall(function()
                            local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                            local char = player.Character
                            
                            if not char or not char.Parent then
                                char = player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
                                task.wait(1)
                                if not char then
                                    return
                                end
                            end
                            
                            local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                            if not hrp then
                                return
                            end
                            
                            local targetPlayer = nil
                            local targetPlayers = {}
                            
                            for _, target in pairs(game:GetService("Players"):GetPlayers()) do
                                if target ~= player and target.Character then
                                    local targetChar = target.Character
                                    if targetChar:FindFirstChild("InLabyrinth") then
                                        table.insert(targetPlayers, target)
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                            
                            if #targetPlayers > 0 then
                                targetPlayer = targetPlayers[math.random(1, #targetPlayers)]
                                
                                if targetPlayer and targetPlayer.Character then
                                    local targetHrp = targetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                    if targetHrp then
                                        hrp.CFrame = targetHrp.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
                                        task.wait(0.2)
                                        
                                        local generalHit = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("GeneralHit")
                                        if generalHit then
                                            local targetPart = targetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or
                                                              targetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or
                                                              targetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
                                            if targetPart then
                                                generalHit:FireServer(targetPart)
                                            end
                                        end
                                        
                                        task.wait(0.5)
                                    end
                                end
                            else
                                local randomX = math.random(-100, 100)
                                local randomZ = math.random(-100, 100)
                                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(randomX, 0, randomZ)
                                task.wait(1)
                            end
                        end)
                        task.wait(1.5)
                    end
                end)

                Fluent:Notify({
                    Title = "Labyrinth Auto Kill",
                    Content = "Started! Targetting players in Labyrinth.",
                    Duration = 3
                })

            else
                if autoLabyrinthLoop then
                    task.cancel(autoLabyrinthLoop)
                    autoLabyrinthLoop = nil
                end
                Fluent:Notify({
                    Title = "Labyrinth Auto Kill",
                    Content = "Stopped!",
                    Duration = 2
                })
            end
        end
    })

    Tab:AddParagraph({
        Title = "2-3 Quests",
        Content = ""
    })

    Tab:AddParagraph({
        Title = "Receiver",
        Content = ""
    })

    local RunReceiver = false
    local RunReceiverLoop = nil

    Tab:AddToggle("IndexForNothing", {
        Title = "Auto Teleport To Safespot + Auto Run Ability (Receiver)",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(Value)
            RunReceiver = Value

            if Value then
                local safespot = workspace:FindFirstChild("Safespot")
                if not safespot then
                    Fluent:Notify({
                        Title = "Error",
                        Content = "Safespot not found! Create Safespot first.",
                        Duration = 3
                    })
                    RunReceiver = false
                    return
                end

                if RunReceiverLoop then
                    task.cancel(RunReceiverLoop)
                    RunReceiverLoop = nil
                end

                RunReceiverLoop = task.spawn(function()
                    local abilityCooldown = 0
                    local positionValue = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)

                    while RunReceiver do
                        pcall(function()
                            local char = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
                            if char then
                                local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                if hrp then
                                    local base = safespot:FindFirstChild("Base")
                                    if base then
                                        if (hrp.Position - base.Position).Magnitude > 10 then
                                            hrp.CFrame = base.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 10, 0)
                                        end
                                    end
                                end

                                if not char:FindFirstChild("entered") then
                                    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(-1209, 328, 3)
                                    task.wait(1)
                                end
                            end

                            abilityCooldown = abilityCooldown + 0.5
                            if abilityCooldown >= 7 then
                                abilityCooldown = 0
                                local generalAbility = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("GeneralAbility")
                                if generalAbility then
                                    local args = { positionValue }
                                    generalAbility:FireServer(unpack(args))
                                end
                            end

                            local char = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
                            if char then
                                local ragdolled = char:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled")
                                if ragdolled and ragdolled.Value == true then
                                    local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                                    if humanoid then
                                        humanoid.Health = 0
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end)
                        task.wait(0.5)
                    end
                end)

                Fluent:Notify({
                    Title = "Run Receiver",
                    Content = "Auto Teleport + Auto Run Ability Enabled!",
                    Duration = 3
                })

            else
                if RunReceiverLoop then
                    task.cancel(RunReceiverLoop)
                    RunReceiverLoop = nil
                end
            end
        end
    })

    Tab:AddParagraph({
        Title = "Helper",
        Content = ""
    })

    local RunHelper = false
    local RunHelperLoop = nil

    Tab:AddToggle("IndexForNothing", {
        Title = "Auto Help Run Player (Helper)",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(Value)
            RunHelper = Value

            if Value then
                local safespot = workspace:FindFirstChild("Safespot")
                if not safespot then
                    Fluent:Notify({
                        Title = "Error",
                        Content = "Safespot not found! Create Safespot first.",
                        Duration = 3
                    })
                    RunHelper = false
                    return
                end

                if RunHelperLoop then
                    task.cancel(RunHelperLoop)
                    RunHelperLoop = nil
                end

                RunHelperLoop = task.spawn(function()
                    while RunHelper do
                        pcall(function()
                            local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                            local char = player.Character

                            if not char or not char.Parent then
                                char = player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
                                task.wait(2)
                                if not char then
                                    return
                                end
                            end

                            local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                            local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                            if not hrp or not humanoid then
                                task.wait(1)
                                return
                            end

                            if not char:FindFirstChild("entered") then
                                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(-1209, 328, 3)
                                task.wait(2)
                                if not char:FindFirstChild("entered") then
                                    task.wait(1)
                                    return
                                end
                            end

                            local base = safespot:FindFirstChild("Base")
                            if base then
                                if (hrp.Position - base.Position).Magnitude > 10 then
                                    hrp.CFrame = base.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 10, 0)
                                end
                            else
                                if (hrp.Position - Vector3.new(10000, -42, 9996)).Magnitude > 5 then
                                    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(10000, -42, 9996)
                                end
                            end

                            task.wait(0.5)

                            local ragdolled = char:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled")
                            if ragdolled and ragdolled.Value == true then
                                humanoid.Health = 0
                                task.wait(2)
                                return
                            end

                            if not char:FindFirstChild("entered") then
                                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(-1209, 328, 3)
                                task.wait(2)
                            end
                        end)
                        task.wait(2)
                    end
                end)

                Fluent:Notify({
                    Title = "Run Helper",
                    Content = "Auto Help Run Player Enabled!",
                    Duration = 3
                })

            else
                if RunHelperLoop then
                    task.cancel(RunHelperLoop)
                    RunHelperLoop = nil
                end
            end
        end
    })


end

-- ============================================================
-- TAB: Frostbite
-- ============================================================
do
    local Tab = Window:AddTab({
        Title = "Frostbite",
        Icon = "rbxassetid://7734068321"
    })

    Tab:AddParagraph({
        Title = "1-2-3-4 Quests",
        Content = ""
    })

    Tab:AddParagraph({
        Title = "Receiver",
        Content = ""
    })

    local frostbiteToggle = false

    Tab:AddToggle("IndexForNothing", {
        Title = "Auto Teleport To Safespot + Auto Use Ability + Slap Aura",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(Value)
            frostbiteToggle = Value
            
            if Value then
                local safespot = workspace:FindFirstChild("Safespot")
                if not safespot then
                    Fluent:Notify({
                        Title = "Error",
                        Content = "Safespot not found! Create Safespot first.",
                        Duration = 3
                    })
                    return false
                end
                
                task.spawn(function()
                    while frostbiteToggle do
                        pcall(function()
                            local char = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
                            if char then
                                local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                if hrp then
                                    local safespot = workspace:FindFirstChild("Safespot")
                                    if safespot then
                                        local base = safespot:FindFirstChild("Base")
                                        if base then
                                            hrp.CFrame = base.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 10, 0)
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                            
                            local generalAbility = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("GeneralAbility")
                            if generalAbility then
                                generalAbility:FireServer(0.012500000651925802)
                            end
                            
                            task.wait(4)
                            
                            local generalHit = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("GeneralHit")
                            if generalHit then
                                local localPlayer = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                                for _, player in pairs(game:GetService("Players"):GetPlayers()) do
                                    if player ~= localPlayer and player.Character then
                                        local targetPart = player.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") or 
                                                          player.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or
                                                          player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                        if targetPart then
                                            generalHit:FireServer(targetPart)
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end)
                        task.wait(0.5)
                    end
                end)
                
                Fluent:Notify({
                    Title = "Frostbite",
                    Content = "Auto Teleport + Ability + Slap Aura Enabled!",
                    Duration = 3
                })
            end
        end
    })

    Tab:AddParagraph({
        Title = "Helper",
        Content = ""
    })

    Tab:AddToggle("IndexForNothing", {
        Title = "Auto Teleport To Receiver + Auto Die On Ragdoll",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(Value)
            if Value then
                task.spawn(function()
                    while Value do
                        pcall(function()
                            local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                            local char = player.Character
                            if not char or not char.Parent then
                                char = player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
                                task.wait(2)
                                if not char then
                                    return
                                end
                            end
                            local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                            local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                            if not hrp or not humanoid then
                                task.wait(1)
                                return
                            end
                            local hasEntered = char:FindFirstChild("entered")
                            if not hasEntered then
                                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(-1209, 328, 3)
                                task.wait(2)
                                hasEntered = char:FindFirstChild("entered")
                                if not hasEntered then
                                    task.wait(1)
                                    return
                                end
                            end
                            local safespot = workspace:FindFirstChild("Safespot")
                            if safespot then
                                local base = safespot:FindFirstChild("Base")
                                if base and (hrp.Position - base.Position).Magnitude > 10 then
                                    hrp.CFrame = base.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 10, 0)
                                end
                            else
                                if (hrp.Position - Vector3.new(10000, -42, 9996)).Magnitude > 5 then
                                    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(10000, -42, 9996)
                                end
                            end
                            task.wait(1)
                            local ragdolled = char:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled")
                            if ragdolled and ragdolled.Value == true then
                                humanoid.Health = 0
                            end
                        end)
                        task.wait(2)
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
    })


end

                            if char then
                                local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                if hrp then
                                    local base = safespot:FindFirstChild("Base")
                                    if base then
                                        if (hrp.Position - base.Position).Magnitude > 10 then
                                            hrp.CFrame = base.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 10, 0)
                                        end
                                    end
                                end

                                    if player ~= localPlayer and player.Character then
                                        local targetPart = player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or 
                                                          player.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or
                                                          player.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
                                        if targetPart then
                                            generalHit:FireServer(targetPart)
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end

                            local char = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
                            if char then
                                local ragdolled = char:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled")
                                if ragdolled and ragdolled.Value == true then
                                    local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                                    if humanoid then
                                        humanoid.Health = 0
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end)
                        task.wait(1.5)

-- TAB: Car Keys

do
    local Tab = Window:AddTab({
        Title = "Car Keys",
        Icon = "rbxassetid://7734068321"
    })

    Tab:AddParagraph({
        Title = "1 Quest",
        Content = ""
    })

    local CarKeysUpgrade = false
    local CarKeysUpgradeLoop = nil

    local attributeFrames = {
        "UtilityAttributeFrame",
        "ArmorAttributeFrame",
        "ReverseAttributeFrame",
        "SteerAngleAttributeFrame",
        "AccelerationAttributeFrame",
        "MaxSpeedAttributeFrame",
        "KnockbackAttributeFrame"
    }

    Tab:AddToggle("IndexForNothing", {
        Title = "Auto Upgrade All Stats",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(Value)
            CarKeysUpgrade = Value

            if Value then
                if CarKeysUpgradeLoop then
                    task.cancel(CarKeysUpgradeLoop)
                    CarKeysUpgradeLoop = nil
                end

                CarKeysUpgradeLoop = task.spawn(function()
                    while CarKeysUpgrade do
                        pcall(function()
                            local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                            local char = player.Character

                            if not char or not char.Parent then
                                char = player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
                                task.wait(2)
                                if not char then
                                    return
                                end
                            end

                            local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                            if not hrp then
                                return
                            end

                            hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(10000, -42, 9996)
                            task.wait(0.5)

                            if not char:FindFirstChild("entered") then
                                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(-1209, 328, 3)
                                task.wait(2)
                            end

                            local carKeysUpgrade = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("CarKeysUpgrade")
                            if carKeysUpgrade then
                                for _, attr in ipairs(attributeFrames) do
                                    local args = { attr }
                                    carKeysUpgrade:FireServer(unpack(args))
                                    task.wait(0.1)
                                end
                            end

                            task.wait(0.5)
                        end)
                        task.wait(1)
                    end
                end)

                Fluent:Notify({
                    Title = "Car Keys Task 1",
                    Content = "Auto Upgrade All Stats Started!",
                    Duration = 3
                })

            else
                if CarKeysUpgradeLoop then
                    task.cancel(CarKeysUpgradeLoop)
                    CarKeysUpgradeLoop = nil
                end
            end
        end
    })

    Tab:AddParagraph({
        Title = "|||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||",
        Content = ""
    })

    Tab:AddParagraph({
        Title = "2-3 Quests",
        Content = ""
    })

    Tab:AddParagraph({
        Title = "Receiver",
        Content = ""
    })

    local CarKeysReceiver = false
    local CarKeysReceiverLoop = nil

    Tab:AddToggle("IndexForNothing", {
        Title = "Auto Teleport To Safespot + Auto Drive Around (Receiver)",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(Value)
            CarKeysReceiver = Value

            if Value then
                local safespot = workspace:FindFirstChild("Safespot")
                if not safespot then
                    Fluent:Notify({
                        Title = "Error",
                        Content = "Safespot not found! Create Safespot first.",
                        Duration = 3
                    })
                    CarKeysReceiver = false
                    return
                end

                if CarKeysReceiverLoop then
                    task.cancel(CarKeysReceiverLoop)
                    CarKeysReceiverLoop = nil
                end

                CarKeysReceiverLoop = task.spawn(function()
                    local drivePositions = {
                        Vector3.new(10000, -42, 9996),
                        Vector3.new(9999, -42, 9991),
                        Vector3.new(10005, -42, 9990),
                        Vector3.new(9995, -42, 10005),
                        Vector3.new(10010, -42, 9995),
                    }
                    local positionIndex = 1

                    while CarKeysReceiver do
                        pcall(function()
                            local char = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
                            if char then
                                local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                if hrp then
                                    local targetPos = drivePositions[positionIndex]
                                    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(targetPos)
                                    positionIndex = positionIndex + 1
                                    if positionIndex > #drivePositions then
                                        positionIndex = 1
                                    end
                                end
                            end

                            local char = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
                            if char and not char:FindFirstChild("entered") then
                                local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                if hrp then
                                    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(-1209, 328, 3)
                                    task.wait(1)
                                end
                            end

                            local virtualInput = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")
                            virtualInput:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game)
                            task.wait(0.1)
                            virtualInput:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game)

                            local char = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
                            if char then
                                local ragdolled = char:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled")
                                if ragdolled and ragdolled.Value == true then
                                    local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                                    if humanoid then
                                        humanoid.Health = 0
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end)
                        task.wait(1.5)
                    end
                end)

                Fluent:Notify({
                    Title = "Car Keys Receiver",
                    Content = "Auto Drive + E Ability Enabled!",
                    Duration = 3
                })

            else
                if CarKeysReceiverLoop then
                    task.cancel(CarKeysReceiverLoop)
                    CarKeysReceiverLoop = nil
                end
            end
        end
    })

    Tab:AddParagraph({
        Title = "Helper",
        Content = ""
    })

    local CarKeysHelper = false
    local CarKeysHelperLoop = nil

    Tab:AddToggle("IndexForNothing", {
        Title = "Auto Help Car Keys Player (Helper)",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(Value)
            CarKeysHelper = Value

            if Value then
                local safespot = workspace:FindFirstChild("Safespot")
                if not safespot then
                    Fluent:Notify({
                        Title = "Error",
                        Content = "Safespot not found! Create Safespot first.",
                        Duration = 3
                    })
                    CarKeysHelper = false
                    return
                end

                if CarKeysHelperLoop then
                    task.cancel(CarKeysHelperLoop)
                    CarKeysHelperLoop = nil
                end

                CarKeysHelperLoop = task.spawn(function()
                    while CarKeysHelper do
                        pcall(function()
                            local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                            local char = player.Character

                            if not char or not char.Parent then
                                char = player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
                                task.wait(2)
                                if not char then
                                    return
                                end
                            end

                            local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                            local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                            if not hrp or not humanoid then
                                task.wait(1)
                                return
                            end

                            if not char:FindFirstChild("entered") then
                                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(-1209, 328, 3)
                                task.wait(2)
                                if not char:FindFirstChild("entered") then
                                    task.wait(1)
                                    return
                                end
                            end

                            local base = safespot:FindFirstChild("Base")
                            if base then
                                if (hrp.Position - base.Position).Magnitude > 10 then
                                    hrp.CFrame = base.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 10, 0)
                                end
                            else
                                if (hrp.Position - Vector3.new(10000, -42, 9996)).Magnitude > 5 then
                                    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(10000, -42, 9996)
                                end
                            end

                            task.wait(0.5)

                            local ragdolled = char:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled")
                            if ragdolled and ragdolled.Value == true then
                                humanoid.Health = 0
                                task.wait(2)
                                return
                            end

                            if not char:FindFirstChild("entered") then
                                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(-1209, 328, 3)
                                task.wait(2)
                            end
                        end)
                        task.wait(2)
                    end
                end)

                Fluent:Notify({
                    Title = "Car Keys Helper",
                    Content = "Auto Help Car Keys Player Enabled!",
                    Duration = 3
                })

            else
                if CarKeysHelperLoop then
                    task.cancel(CarKeysHelperLoop)
                    CarKeysHelperLoop = nil
                end
            end
        end
    })


end

-- TAB: Ultra Instinct
-- ============================================================
do
    local Tab = Window:AddTab({
        Title = "Ultra Instinct",
        Icon = "rbxassetid://7734068321"
    })

    Tab:AddParagraph({
        Title = "1-2 Quest",
        Content = ""
    })

    Tab:AddParagraph({
        Title = "Receiver",
        Content = ""
    })

    local uiToggle = false

    Tab:AddToggle("IndexForNothing", {
        Title = "Auto Teleport To Safespot + Slap Aura",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(Value)
            uiToggle = Value
            
            if Value then
                local safespot = workspace:FindFirstChild("Safespot")
                if not safespot then
                    Fluent:Notify({
                        Title = "Error",
                        Content = "Safespot not found! Create Safespot first.",
                        Duration = 3
                    })
                    return false
                end
                
                task.spawn(function()
                    while uiToggle do
                        pcall(function()
                            local char = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
                            if char then
                                local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                if hrp then
                                    local safespot = workspace:FindFirstChild("Safespot")
                                    if safespot then
                                        local base = safespot:FindFirstChild("Base")
                                        if base then
                                            hrp.CFrame = base.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 10, 0)
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                            
                            local generalHit = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("GeneralHit")
                            if generalHit then
                                local localPlayer = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                                for _, player in pairs(game:GetService("Players"):GetPlayers()) do
                                    if player ~= localPlayer and player.Character then
                                        local targetPart = player.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") or 
                                                          player.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or
                                                          player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                        if targetPart then
                                            generalHit:FireServer(targetPart)
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end)
                        task.wait(0.5)
                    end
                end)
                
                Fluent:Notify({
                    Title = "Ultra Instinct",
                    Content = "Auto Teleport + Slap Aura Enabled!",
                    Duration = 3
                })
            end
        end
    })

    Tab:AddParagraph({
        Title = "Helper",
        Content = ""
    })

    Tab:AddToggle("IndexForNothing", {
        Title = "Auto Teleport To Receiver + Slap Aura",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(Value)
            if Value then
                task.spawn(function()
                    while Value do
                        pcall(function()
                            local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                            local char = player.Character
                            if not char or not char.Parent then
                                char = player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
                                task.wait(2)
                                if not char then
                                    return
                                end
                            end
                            local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                            local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                            if not hrp or not humanoid then
                                task.wait(1)
                                return
                            end
                            local glove = player.leaderstats and player.leaderstats:FindFirstChild("Glove")
                            if glove and glove.Value ~= "Default" then
                                Fluent:Notify({
                                    Title = "Error",
                                    Content = "Equip Default Glove",
                                    Duration = 3
                                })
                                return
                            end
                            local hasEntered = char:FindFirstChild("entered")
                            if not hasEntered then
                                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(-1209, 328, 3)
                                task.wait(2)
                                hasEntered = char:FindFirstChild("entered")
                                if not hasEntered then
                                    task.wait(1)
                                    return
                                end
                            end
                            local safespot = workspace:FindFirstChild("Safespot")
                            if safespot then
                                local base = safespot:FindFirstChild("Base")
                                if base and (hrp.Position - base.Position).Magnitude > 10 then
                                    hrp.CFrame = base.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 10, 0)
                                end
                            else
                                if (hrp.Position - Vector3.new(10000, -42, 9996)).Magnitude > 5 then
                                    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(10000, -42, 9996)
                                end
                            end
                            task.wait(1)
                            local ragdolled = char:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled")
                            if ragdolled and ragdolled.Value == true then
                                humanoid.Health = 0
                                task.wait(1)
                                return
                            end
                            local generalHit = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("GeneralHit")
                            if generalHit then
                                for _, player in pairs(game:GetService("Players"):GetPlayers()) do
                                    if player ~= game.Players.LocalPlayer and player.Character then
                                        local targetPart = player.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") or player.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                        if targetPart then
                                            generalHit:FireServer(targetPart)
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end)
                        task.wait(1.5)
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
    })


end

    -- Store owned badges
    local ownedBadges = {}

    -- Fetch owned badges from ReplicatedStorage
    local function fetchOwnedBadges()
        ownedBadges = {}
        
        local playerData = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("PlayerData")
        if not playerData then
            return false
        end
        
        local playerFolder = playerData:FindFirstChild(username)
        if not playerFolder then
            return false
        end
        
        local unlockedBadges = playerFolder:FindFirstChild("UnlockedBadges")
        if not unlockedBadges then
            return false
        end
        
        if unlockedBadges:IsA("StringValue") then
            local badgeIdsString = unlockedBadges.Value
            
            if badgeIdsString and badgeIdsString ~= "" then
                local trimmed = badgeIdsString:sub(2, -2)
                
                for id in string.gmatch(trimmed, "[^,]+") do
                    local cleanId = id:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
                    ownedBadges[cleanId] = true
                end
            end
            return true
        end
        return false
    end

    local WebhookSettings = {
        WebhookURL = "",
        SendInterval = "No time",
        LoggedObjects = {},
        SlapsThreshold = 0,
        NullShardsThreshold = 0,
        Enabled = false,
        PingType = "No Pings",
        UserID = "",
        RoleID = "",
        
        LastSlapsValue = 0,
        LastNullShardsValue = 0,
        TrackedSlaps = false,
        TrackedNullShards = false,
        SlapsStartValue = 0,
        NullShardsStartValue = 0,
        IsSlapsTracking = false,
        IsNullShardsTracking = false,
        ObjectLogged = {},
        SlapsLogged = false,
        NullShardsLogged = false,
        SlappleLogged = {},
        GoldenSlappleLogged = false,
        LastBadgeCheck = {},
        BadgeLogSent = false,
    }

    -- Helper function to get Null Shards
    function WebhookSettings:GetNullShards()
        local playerData = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("PlayerData")
        if playerData then
            local userData = playerData:FindFirstChild(username)
            if userData then
                local nullShards = userData:FindFirstChild("NullShards")
                if nullShards then
                    return nullShards.Value
                end
            end
        end
        return 0
    end

    -- Get ping prefix based on settings
    local function getPingPrefix()
        if WebhookSettings.PingType == "No Pings" then
            return ""
        elseif WebhookSettings.PingType == "@User Ping" and WebhookSettings.UserID ~= "" then
            return "<@" .. WebhookSettings.UserID .. "> "
        elseif WebhookSettings.PingType == "@Role Ping" and WebhookSettings.RoleID ~= "" then
            return "<@&" .. WebhookSettings.RoleID .. "> "
        elseif WebhookSettings.PingType == "@Everyone Ping" then
            return "@everyone "
        elseif WebhookSettings.PingType == "@Here Ping" then
            return "@here "
        end
        return ""
    end

    -- Webhook send function
    local function sendWebhookMessage(content)
        if not WebhookSettings.Enabled or WebhookSettings.WebhookURL == "" then return end
        
        local ping = getPingPrefix()
        local fullContent = ping .. content
        
        local payload = {
            content = fullContent or "No message provided"
        }
        
        local jsonData = HttpService:JSONEncode(payload)
        
        local success, response = pcall(function()
            if syn and syn.request then
                return syn.request({
                    Url = WebhookSettings.WebhookURL,
                    Method = "POST",
                    Headers = {
                        ["Content-Type"] = "application/json"
                    },
                    Body = jsonData
                })
            elseif http_request then
                return http_request({
                    Url = WebhookSettings.WebhookURL,
                    Method = "POST",
                    Headers = {
                        ["Content-Type"] = "application/json"
                    },
                    Body = jsonData
                })
            elseif request then
                return request({
                    Url = WebhookSettings.WebhookURL,
                    Method = "POST",
                    Headers = {
                        ["Content-Type"] = "application/json"
                    },
                    Body = jsonData
                })
            else
                warn("No request method available for webhook!")
                return nil, "No request method available"
            end
        end)
        
        if not success then
            warn("Failed to send webhook: " .. tostring(response))
        end
    end

    -- 1. Webhook URL Input
    Tab:AddInput("IndexForNothing", {
        Title = "Webhook URL",
        Description = "Enter your Discord webhook URL",
        Default = "",
        Numeric = false,
        Finished = false,
        Callback = function(Value)
            WebhookSettings.WebhookURL = Value
        end
    })

    -- 2. Send Interval Dropdown

        Title = "Send Interval",
        Values = {"No time", "30 Seconds", "1 Minute", "10 Minutes", "30 Minutes", "1 Hour", "5 Hours", "10 Hours", "1 Day"},
        Default = "No time",
        Callback = function(Value)
            WebhookSettings.SendInterval = Value
        end
    })

    -- 3. Multi Dropdown for Objects
    Tab:AddDropdown("IndexForNothing", {
        Title = "Choose Objects Spawns To Log",
        Values = {"Jet Orb", "Phase Orb", "Glitch Orb", "Siphon Orb", "MATERIALIZE Orb", "Slapple", "Golden Slapple", "Toolbox"},
        Multi = true,
        Default = {},
        Callback = function(Values)
            WebhookSettings.LoggedObjects = Values
            WebhookSettings.ObjectLogged = {}
            WebhookSettings.SlappleLogged = {}
            WebhookSettings.GoldenSlappleLogged = false
            for _, obj in pairs(Values) do
                if obj == "Slapple" then
                    WebhookSettings.SlappleLogged[obj] = {}
                elseif obj == "Golden Slapple" then
                    WebhookSettings.GoldenSlappleLogged = false
                else
                    WebhookSettings.ObjectLogged[obj] = false
                end
            end
        end
    })

    -- 4. Slaps Threshold Dropdown
    Tab:AddDropdown("IndexForNothing", {
        Title = "Slaps Threshold",
        Values = {"100+ Slaps", "1000+ Slaps", "10000+ Slaps", "100000+ Slaps", "1000000+ Slaps", "Custom Slaps Amount"},
        Default = "1000+ Slaps",
        Callback = function(Value)
            if Value == "Custom Slaps Amount" then
                WebhookSettings.SlapsThreshold = 0
            else
                local num = tonumber(Value:match("(%d+)")) or 0
                WebhookSettings.SlapsThreshold = num
                WebhookSettings.TrackedSlaps = false
                WebhookSettings.SlapsLogged = false
            end
        end
    })
